### [38] [Dynamic-TD3: A Novel Algorithm for UAV Path Planning with Dynamic Obstacle Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00059)
*Wentao Chen,Jingtang Chen,Mingjian Fu,Tiantian Li,Youfeng Su,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic-TD3框架，通过自适应轨迹关系演化机制（ATREM）和物理感知门控卡尔曼滤波（PAG-KF）解决无人机导航中的安全-探索困境，在复杂动态威胁环境中实现严格安全约束与机动性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在无人机自主导航中面临安全-探索困境：软惩罚机制鼓励冒险试错，而基于约束的方法在传感器噪声和意图不确定性下性能下降。

Method: 将导航建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），集成自适应轨迹关系演化机制（ATREM）捕捉长程意图，采用物理感知门控卡尔曼滤波（PAG-KF）缓解非平稳观测噪声，通过拉格朗日松弛构建双准则策略平衡任务效率与硬安全约束。

Result: 在激进动态威胁实验中，该方法展现出更优的碰撞规避性能、更低的能耗和更平滑的飞行轨迹。

Conclusion: Dynamic-TD3框架能有效解决DRL在无人机导航中的安全-探索困境，在保证严格安全约束的同时维持机动性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) finds extensive application in autonomous drone navigation within complex, high-risk environments. However, its practical deployment faces a safety-exploration dilemma: soft penalty mechanisms encourage risky trial-and-error, while most constraint-based methods suffer degraded performance under sensor noise and intent uncertainty. We propose Dynamic-TD3, a physically enhanced framework that enforces strict safety constraints while maintaining maneuverability by modeling navigation as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP). This framework integrates an Adaptive Trajectory Relational Evolution Mechanism (ATREM) to capture long-range intentions and employs a Physically Aware Gated Kalman Filter (PAG-KF) to mitigate non-stationary observation noise. The resulting state representation drives a dual-criterion policy that balances mission efficiency against hard safety constraints via Lagrangian relaxation. In experiments with aggressive dynamic threats, this approach demonstrates superior collision avoidance performance, reduced energy consumption, and smoother flight trajectories.

</details>


### [39] [Do Open-Loop Metrics Predict Closed-Loop Driving? A Cross-Benchmark Correlation Study of NAVSIM and Bench2Drive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00066)
*Yiru Wang,Anqing Jiang,Shuo Wang,Yuwen Heng,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了15种SOTA方法的NAVSIM（开环）和Bench2Drive（闭环）指标，发现NAVSIM v2的安全感知指标未能可靠预测闭环性能。主要发现：(1) PDM分数与Drive分数强相关但非单调；(2) Ego Progress是闭环成功的最强单预测因子；(3) 开环中安全优先的方法在闭环中因超时/慢速惩罚表现不佳。简化3指标公式可达到与5指标PDMS相同的预测能力（Spearman ρ=0.90）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开环评估速度快、可复现，但其能否预测真实闭环驾驶性能一直存疑。已有研究显示传统开环指标（ADE、FDE）与闭环驾驶分数无可靠关联。本文旨在探究NAVSIM v2引入的更新的、关注安全的开环指标是否能弥合这一差距。

Method: 系统交叉比对15种SOTA方法在NAVSIM（开环）和Bench2Drive（闭环）上的已发表结果，构建配对数据集（8种方法有完整配对数据）。通过Spearman相关性分析、排名反转分析等方法研究开环子指标与闭环性能之间的关系。

Result: (1) PDM分数与Driving Score强正相关但非单调，存在明显排名反转；(2) Ego Progress是闭环成功的最强单预测因子，远超碰撞指标NC；(3) 安全-进展权衡在开环和闭环中表现不同：安全优先方法在NAVSIM排名高但在闭环中因超时/慢速惩罚表现差；(4) 简化3指标公式（EP+NC+Progress）与5指标PDMS预测能力相同（ρ=0.90）；(5) 雪球效应（开环小偏差累积为闭环失败）是残差差距的候选机制。

Conclusion: NAVSIM v2的安全感知开环指标仍无法可靠预测闭环驾驶性能。Ego Progress是最强的单指标预测因子，但单调性不足。当前SOTA方法中TTC和Comfort趋于饱和，贡献有限。开环评估需要更好的机制来捕捉闭环中的雪球效应和安全-进展的动态权衡。

Abstract: Open-loop evaluation offers fast, reproducible assessment of autonomous driving planners, but its ability to predict real closed-loop driving performance remains questionable. Prior work has shown that traditional open-loop metrics such as Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) exhibit no reliable correlation with closed-loop Driving Score. In this paper, we ask whether the more recent, safety-aware open-loop metrics introduced by NAVSIM~v2 can bridge this gap. By systematically cross-referencing published results from 15 state-of-the-art methods across NAVSIM (open-loop) and Bench2Drive (closed-loop), we compile a paired dataset of open-loop sub-metrics and closed-loop performance, yielding 8 methods with complete paired data. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) the aggregate NAVSIM PDM Score shows a strong positive but non-monotonic correlation with Bench2Drive Driving Score, with clear ranking inversions; (2) among individual NAVSIM sub-metrics, Ego Progress (EP) is the strongest single predictor of closed-loop success, substantially exceeding the safety-critical collision metric NC; (3) the safety-progress trade-off manifests differently in open-loop and closed-loop: methods that maximize safety at the expense of progress rank highly in NAVSIM but underperform in closed-loop due to timeout and slow-driving penalties. We further demonstrate that a much simpler 3-metric formula matches the predictive power of the full 5-metric PDMS at the same Spearman $ρ{=}0.90$ on our paired sample of $n{=}8$ methods, suggesting that within current state-of-the-art methods -- where TTC and Comfort approach saturation -- these two sub-metrics add little marginal information for closed-loop ranking. Additionally, we identify the snowball effect -- where small open-loop deviations compound into closed-loop failures -- as a candidate mechanism for the residual gap.

</details>


### [40] [Being-H0.7: A Latent World-Action Model from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00078)
*Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Yicheng Feng,Sipeng Zheng,Haiweng Xu,Chaoyi Xu,Ziheng Xi,Yuhui Fu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Being-H0.7是一种潜在世界-动作模型，通过在VLA策略中插入可学习的潜在查询作为推理接口，并采用未来感知的双分支设计（先验分支和后验分支），在不生成未来帧的情况下实现未来感知推理，在6个仿真基准和多种真实世界任务中达到SOTA或相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型由于稀疏的动作监督，倾向于学习捷径映射而非真正的动力学、接触和任务进展表征；而世界-动作模型通过像素空间视频预测引入未来推理，但代价高昂且可能建模与动作生成无关的视觉细节。

Method: 提出Being-H0.7，在感知和动作之间插入可学习潜在查询作为紧凑推理接口，采用双分支设计：可部署的先验分支从当前上下文推断潜在状态，仅训练使用的后验分支用未来观测嵌入替换查询，在潜在推理空间中对齐两个分支，使先验分支学会从当前观测推理未来感知且动作有用的结构。

Result: 在6个仿真基准和多种真实世界任务中达到SOTA或相当性能，兼具世界模型的预测优势与直接VLA策略的高效可部署性。

Conclusion: Being-H0.7验证了在潜在空间中实现未来感知推理的可行性，在不进行视觉展开的情况下结合了世界模型的预测能力和VLA策略的部署效率。

Abstract: Visual-Language-Action models (VLAs) have advanced generalist robot control by mapping multimodal observations and language instructions directly to actions, but sparse action supervision often encourages shortcut mappings rather than representations of dynamics, contact, and task progress. Recent world-action models introduce future prediction through video rollouts, yet pixel-space prediction is a costly and indirect substrate for control, as it may model visual details irrelevant to action generation and introduces substantial training or inference overhead. We present Being-H0.7, a latent world-action model that brings future-aware reasoning into VLA-style policies without generating future frames. Being-H0.7 inserts learnable latent queries between perception and action as a compact reasoning interface, and trains them with a future-informed dual-branch design: a deployable prior branch infers latent states from the current context, while a training-only posterior branch replaces the queries with embeddings from future observations. Jointly aligning the two branches at the latent reasoning space leads the prior branch to reason future-aware, action-useful structure from current observations alone. At inference, Being-H0.7 discards the posterior branch and performs no visual rollout. Experiments across six simulation benchmarks and diverse real-world tasks show that Being-H0.7 achieves state-of-the-art or comparable performance, combining the predictive benefits of world models with the efficiency and deployability of direct VLA policies.

</details>


### [41] [World Model for Robot Learning: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00080)
*Bohan Hou,Gen Li,Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Sicong Leng,Haoran Geng,Yanjie Ze,Tatsuya Harada,Philip Torr,Oier Mees,Marc Pollefeys,Zhuang Liu,Jiajun Wu,Pieter Abbeel,Jitendra Malik,Yilun Du,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器人学习中世界模型的研究进展，涵盖其与策略的耦合方式、作为学习模拟器的应用、视频世界模型的发展，以及在导航和自动驾驶中的实践，并总结了代表性数据集、基准和评估协议，最后指出了主要挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的世界模型研究在架构、功能角色和具身应用领域方面分散且碎片化，缺乏系统性梳理，因此需要一篇从机器人学习视角出发的全面综述来整合现有工作。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，从机器人学习视角出发，对世界模型的架构、功能角色（策略耦合、学习模拟器、视频生成）及具体应用领域（导航、自动驾驶）进行分类梳理，并总结代表性数据集、基准和评估协议。

Result: 梳理了世界模型与机器人策略的多种耦合方式，总结了世界模型作为强化学习和评估的学习模拟器的角色，归纳了机器人视频世界模型从想象生成到可控、结构化、基础模型规模的发展历程，并关联了导航和自动驾驶领域的应用，汇总了代表性数据集、基准和评估协议。

Conclusion: 该综述系统整合了机器人学习中世界模型的快速增长文献，厘清了关键范式和应用，指出了预测建模在具身智能体中的主要挑战和未来方向，并将持续维护配套的GitHub仓库以跟踪最新进展。

Abstract: World models, which are predictive representations of how environments evolve under actions, have become a central component of robot learning. They support policy learning, planning, simulation, evaluation, data generation, and have advanced rapidly with the rise of foundation models and large-scale video generation. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, functional roles, and embodied application domains. To address this gap, we present a comprehensive review of world models from a robot-learning perspective. We examine how world models are coupled with robot policies, how they serve as learned simulators for reinforcement learning and evaluation, and how robotic video world models have progressed from imagination-based generation to controllable, structured, and foundation-scale formulations. We further connect these ideas to navigation and autonomous driving, and summarize representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols. Overall, this survey systematically reviews the rapidly growing literature on world models for robot learning, clarifies key paradigms and applications, and highlights major challenges and future directions for predictive modeling in embodied agents. To facilitate continued access to newly emerging works, benchmarks, and resources, we will maintain and regularly update the accompanying GitHub repository alongside this survey.

</details>


### [42] [Predictive Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs for Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00121)
*Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Charlie Gauthier,Kumaraditya Gupta,Shima Shahfar,Kirsty Ellis,Steven Parkison,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Perpetua*滤波器与PredictiveGraphs框架，实现了对动态环境中物体状态的时间-空间-语义联合推理与未来状态预测，在仿真和真实世界的半静态变化场景中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数空间-语义表示方法缺乏跨时间推理能力，无法处理环境中物体在多次观测间发生的结构化变化（如杯子在厨房中的周期性移动），因此需要一种能够同时进行时间、空间和语义推理的方法。

Method: 提出Perpetua*滤波器，在观测到的环境状态上执行贝叶斯推理；将其集成到名为PredictiveGraphs的3D场景图结构中，图中节点表示物体，边编码为Perpetua*滤波器以建模空间-语义关系。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的动态导航任务中验证，真实实验在为期三周、每两小时发生半静态变化的环境中进行。方法在预测未来环境状态方面优于基线，即使存在分布偏移也能保持性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了时间-空间-语义联合推理，为机器人在长期动态环境中进行状态预测提供有效方案。

Abstract: We have seen tremendous recent progress in our ability to build "spatio-semantic" representations that enable robots to perform complex reasoning across geometry and semantics. However, the vast majority of these methods lack any ability to perform reasoning across time. This is a desirable property in situations where a robot repeatedly observes an environment where instances may change in between observations, but in a structured way. Consider as an example a home environment where the location of a mug typically moves from the cupboard to a countertop to the sink and then back to the cupboard on a daily basis. We should be able to learn this cyclic behavior and use it to predict the state of the mug in the future. In this work, we propose a method that is able to perform this type of tempo-spatio-semantic reasoning. Underpinning the method is a filter, Perpetua$^*$, that performs Bayesian reasoning on the states of the environment that are observed over time. This filter is integrated within a 3D scene graph structure that we call PredictiveGraphs, where nodes represent objects and edges function as Perpetua$^*$ filters encoding spatio-semantic relationships. We validate the method in both simulation and real-world dynamic navigation tasks, where our real world experiments consist of an environment that is undergoing semi-static changes at a bi-hourly frequency over a period of three weeks. In both settings, we demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines in predicting future environment states, even in the presence of distributional shifts.

</details>


### [43] [E$^2$DT: Efficient and Effective Decision Transformer with Experience-Aware Sampling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00159)
*Kaiyan Zhao,Borong Zhang,Yiming Wang,Xingyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Yuyang Chen,Xiaoguang Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 E²DT 框架，通过基于 k-行列式点过程的质量-多样性联合核函数，实现决策Transformer的主动经验选择，在机器人操作任务中提升样本效率和学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Decision Transformer 依赖均匀回放，缺乏主动探索机制，导致样本效率低、探索受限、整体效果差；而过量探索虽能避免局部最优，但会延迟策略收敛、降低效率。

Method: 提出 E²DT 框架，利用 DT 内部潜在嵌入度量轨迹窗口间的多样性，通过 RTG 分位数、预测不确定性和逆频次阶段覆盖的复合指标量化质量，将两者融合为质量-多样性联合核函数，采用 k-DPP 采样优先选择高回报、高不确定性和代表性不足的轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作基准上，E²DT 持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 将策略学习与经验感知采样相结合，为鲁棒的长期机器人学习提供了一条原则性路径。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation, the Decision Transformer (DT) has emerged as an effective framework for addressing long-horizon tasks. However, DT's performance depends heavily on the coverage of collected experiences. Without an active exploration mechanism, standard DT relies on uniform replay, which leads to poor sample efficiency, limited exploration, and reduced overall effectiveness. At the same time, while excessive exploration can help avoid local optima, it often delays policy convergence and leads to degraded efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose E$^2$DT, a DT-guided k-Determinantal Point Process sampling framework that enables the model to actively shape its own experience selection. Our framework is experience-aware, allowing E$^2$DT to be both efficient, by prioritizing sampling quality, such as high-return, high-uncertainty, and underrepresented trajectories, and effective, by ensuring diversity across trajectory windows to preserve policy optimality. Specifically, DT's internal latent embeddings measure diversity across trajectory windows, while quality is quantified through a composite metric that integrates return-to-go (RTG) quantiles, predictive uncertainty, and stage coverage based on inverse frequency. These two dimensions are integrated into a novel quality-diversity joint kernel that prioritizes the most informative experiences, thereby enabling learning that is both efficient and effective. We evaluate E$^2$DT on challenging robotic manipulation benchmarks in both simulation and real-robot settings. Results show that it consistently outperforms prior methods. These findings demonstrate that coupling policy learning with experience-aware sampling provides a principled path toward robust long-horizon robotic learning.

</details>


### [44] [Lucid-XR: An Extended-Reality Data Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00244)
*Yajvan Ravan,Adam Rashid,Alan Yu,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Zhutian Yang,Qinxi Yu,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lucid-XR 是一个生成式数据引擎，通过 XR 头显上的 Web 物理模拟环境和自然语言驱动的视频生成流水线，为机器人系统生成多样化、逼真的多模态训练数据，在零样本迁移条件下实现了对未见过的杂乱和低光照环境的有效策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统依赖真实世界数据收集，成本高、泛化性差，缺乏一个可大规模访问、无需专用设备、能生成多样化逼真数据以训练真实世界机器人系统的数据引擎。

Method: 提出 Lucid-XR 系统，核心组件包括：1) vuer——基于 Web 的物理模拟环境，直接运行于 XR 头显，支持互联网级沉浸式低延迟虚拟交互；2) 设备端物理模拟结合人体到机器人姿态重定向；3) 自然语言可控的物理引导视频生成流水线，用于数据增强。

Result: 完全使用 Lucid-XR 合成数据训练的机器人视觉策略，能够在零样本条件下成功迁移到未见过的、杂乱的、光照条件差的评估环境，覆盖涉及软材料、松散颗粒和刚体接触的灵巧操作任务。

Conclusion: Lucid-XR 验证了使用合成数据进行机器人视觉策略训练并实现零样本迁移的可行性，为机器人系统的低成本、大规模数据生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We introduce Lucid-XR, a generative data engine for creating diverse and realistic-looking multi-modal data to train real-world robotic systems. At the core of Lucid-XR is vuer, a web-based physics simulation environment that runs directly on the XR headset, enabling internet-scale access to immersive, latency-free virtual interactions without requiring specialized equipment. The complete system integrates on-device physics simulation with human-to-robot pose retargeting. Data collected is further amplified by a physics-guided video generation pipeline steerable via natural language specifications. We demonstrate zero-shot transfer of robot visual policies to unseen, cluttered, and badly lit evaluation environments, after training entirely on Lucid-XR's synthetic data. We include examples across dexterous manipulation tasks that involve soft materials, loosely bound particles, and rigid body contact. Project website: https://lucidxr.github.io

</details>


### [45] [Task-Conditioned Uncertainty Costmaps for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00261)
*Kartikeya Singh,Christo Aluckal,Romeo Orsolino,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认知不确定性建模的足式机器人落足点预测方法，通过将不确定性融入代价地图生成框架，实现了对分布外地形的有效检测，并在仿真和真实实验中最高降低37%的可行性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在高度非结构化地形中，从感知输入（如高度扫描）准确预测未来接触点仍然具有挑战性，现有方法在分布外地形上表现不佳。

Method: 对预测落足点中的认知不确定性进行建模，该不确定性以地形观测和指令运动为条件，从而区分分布内与分布外运行状态；将学习到的不确定性用于检测OOD区域，并融入统一的代价地图生成框架，实现不确定性感知的路径规划。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，该方法在OOD检测上表现提升，仿真可行性误差最多降低37%，相比仅基于几何信息的基线方法展现出更可靠的规划行为。

Conclusion: 通过建模认知不确定性，单一学习模型能够表达因训练覆盖不足导致的不确定性，从而实现更鲁棒的分布外地形适应与路径规划。

Abstract: Legged robots maintain dynamic feasibility through multicontact interactions with terrain. Learned foothold prediction can provide feasibility-aware costs for motion planning and path selection, but accurately predicting future contacts from perceptual inputs such as height scans remains challenging on highly unstructured terrain, even with a repetitive gait cycle. In this work, we show that modeling epistemic uncertainty in predicted footholds, conditioned on terrain observations and commanded motion, distinguishes in-distribution from out-of-distribution operating regimes in simulation and real-world settings. This allows a single learned model, trained on limited data distributions, to express uncertainty caused by missing training coverage. We use this learned uncertainty to detect OOD regions and incorporate them into a unified costmap-generation framework for uncertainty-aware path planning. Using these uncertainty-aware costmaps, we evaluate feasibility error across in-distribution and OOD terrains in simulation and real-world settings. The results show improved OOD detection, up to a 37% reduction in simulation feasibility error, and more reliable planning behavior than geometry-only baselines.

</details>

*Kaiwen Zuo,Shuyuan Yang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Grasp force estimation can help prevent robots from damaging delicate objects during manipulation and improve learning-based robotic control. Integrating force sensing into deformable grippers negotiates trade-offs in cost, complexity, mechanical robustness, and performance. With the growing integration of RGB-D wrist cameras into robotic systems for control purposes, camera-based techniques are a promising solution for indirect visual force estimation. Current approaches mostly utilize end-to-end deep learning, which can be brittle when generalizing to new scenarios, while existing model-based approaches are unsuited to grasping and modern grasper geometries. To address these challenges, we developed a model-based visual force sensing approach integrating an iterative contact localization with generalization to unseen objects. The system extracts structural key points from wrist camera RGB-D images of deforming fin-ray-shaped soft grippers, and uses these key points to define parameters of an inverse finite element analysis simulation in Simulation Open Framework Architecture. The iterative contact localization sub-system utilizes a deep learning-based online 3D reconstruction and pose estimation pipeline to dynamically update contact location, and is robust to visual occlusion and unseen objects. Our system demonstrated an average root mean square error of 0.23 N and normalized root mean square deviation of 2.11% during the load phase, and 0.48 N and 4.34% over the entire grasping process when interacting with different objects under various conditions, showcasing its potential for real-time model-based indirect force sensing of soft grippers.

</details>


### [47] [PrefMoE: Robust Preference Modeling with Mixture-of-Experts Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00384)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Dezhong Zhao,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PrefMoE框架，通过混合专家（MoE）架构学习多个专用奖励专家，并采用轨迹级软路由自适应组合，解决偏好强化学习中异构、冲突偏好数据导致的单奖励模型鲁棒性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模偏好数据集常包含标注者间分歧和标注者内不一致等异构且部分冲突的监督信号，现有单奖励模型方法被迫对这些不兼容信号取平均，导致鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出PrefMoE框架：1) 学习多个专用奖励专家（Experts）；2) 采用轨迹级软路由（Soft Routing）自适应组合专家输出；3) 引入负载均衡正则化防止专家坍缩，稳定训练。

Result: 在D4RL locomotion和MetaWorld操作任务上，PrefMoE相比强单模型基线提升了偏好预测鲁棒性，并得到更可靠的下游策略学习效果。

Conclusion: PrefMoE通过混合专家架构有效建模噪声和异构偏好监督下的多样化潜在偏好模式，为偏好强化学习提供了一种鲁棒的奖励学习方案。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative to manual reward engineering by learning reward structures from comparative feedback. However, large-scale preference datasets, whether collected from crowdsourced annotators or generated by synthetic teachers, often contain heterogeneous and partially conflicting supervision, including disagreement across annotators and inconsistency within annotators. Existing reward learning methods typically fit a single reward model to such data, forcing it to average incompatible signals and thereby limiting robustness. To solve this, we propose PrefMoE, a mixture-of-experts reward learning framework for robust preference modeling. PrefMoE learns multiple specialized reward experts and uses trajectory-level soft routing to combine them adaptively, enabling the model to capture diverse latent preference patterns under noisy and heterogeneous preference supervision. A load-balancing regularizer further stabilizes training by preventing expert collapse. Across locomotion benchmarks from D4RL and manipulation tasks from MetaWorld, PrefMoE improves preference prediction robustness and leads to more reliable downstream policy learning than strong single-model baselines.

</details>


### [48] [MiniVLA-Nav v1: A Multi-Scene Simulation Dataset for Language-Conditioned Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00397)
*Ali Al-Bustami,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniVLA-Nav v1 是一个用于语言条件物体接近（LCOA）导航任务的仿真数据集，包含1,174个导航轨迹，在四个Isaac Sim场景中，使用Nova Carter机器人依据自然语言指令导航至目标物体并停在1米范围内，提供RGB图像、深度图、实例分割掩码及专家动作标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航数据集在语言指令与视觉感知的细粒度对齐、动作标签的规范性以及轨迹多样性方面存在不足，缺乏面向"语言条件物体接近"这一具体任务的高质量仿真数据集，难以支持VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的训练与评估。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中构建Office、Hospital、Full Warehouse、Warehouse with Multiple Shelves四个逼真环境；使用Nova Carter差分驱动机器人，基于视觉比例控制器以60Hz频率采集连续(v,omega)动作及7x7 tokenized expert标签；设计三种生成距离层级（近/中/远）、12类物体类别、18个训练模板与12个释义OOD模板，生成1,174条带有同步RGB图像（640x640）、深度图、实例分割掩码的多模态导航轨迹。

Result: 成功构建包含1,174个episode的多模态导航数据集，轨迹生成距离与轨迹长度高度相关（Pearson r=0.94），提供五个评估划分以支持分布内准确率、模板释义鲁棒性和OOD物体类别基准测试。

Conclusion: MiniVLA-Nav v1为LCOA导航任务提供了一个标准化、多样化且公开可用的仿真数据集，有助于推动VLA模型在语言引导自主导航方面的研究。数据集已在Hugging Face上开源。

Abstract: We present MiniVLA-Nav v1, a simulation dataset for Language-Conditioned Object Approach (LCOA) navigation: given a short natural-language instruction, an NVIDIA Nova Carter differential-drive robot must navigate to the named object and stop within 1 m across four photorealistic Isaac Sim environments (Office, Hospital, Full Warehouse, and Warehouse with Multiple Shelves). Each of the 1,174 episodes pairs an instruction with synchronized 640x640 RGB images, metric depth maps (float32, metres), and instance segmentation masks, together with continuous (v,omega) and 7x7 tokenized expert action labels recorded at 60 Hz from a vision-based proportional controller. Trajectory diversity is ensured through three spawn-distance tiers (near: 1.5-3.5 m, mid: 3.5-7.0 m, far: global curated points; Pearson r=0.94 between spawn distance and trajectory length), 12 object categories, 18 training templates, and 12 paraphrase-OOD templates. Five evaluation splits support in-distribution accuracy, template-paraphrase robustness, and OOD object-category benchmarking. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/alibustami/miniVLA-Nav

</details>


### [49] [Learning while Deploying: Fleet-Scale Reinforcement Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00416)
*Yi Wang,Xinchen Li,Pengwei Xie,Pu Yang,Buqing Nie,Yunuo Cai,Qinglin Zhang,Chendi Qu,Jeffrey Wu,Jianheng Song,Xinlin Ren,Jingshun Huang,Mingjie Pan,Siyuan Feng,Zhi Chen,Jianlan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LWD框架，通过离线到在线的强化学习，利用机器人集群的自主 rollout 和人工干预数据，持续后训练通用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在16台双臂机器人、8项真实操作任务上达到95%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线预训练的通用机器人策略在真实部署中面临分布偏移、长尾故障、任务变化等问题，纯离线数据不足以支撑鲁棒的部署表现，需要一种能在部署过程中持续学习的框架。

Method: LWD框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 集群部署、共享物理经验、策略改进和重新部署的闭环；(2) Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) 用于鲁棒的价值估计；(3) Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) 用于从基于流的VLA动作生成器中提取策略。

Result: 在16台双臂机器人、8项真实世界操作任务（包括语义杂货补货和3-5分钟长时域任务）上验证，单一通用策略随集群经验积累持续提升，达到95%平均成功率，长时域任务提升最显著。

Conclusion: LWD有效弥合了离线预训练与在线部署之间的差距，证明通过集群规模的在线强化学习可以持续改进通用VLA策略，为机器人策略的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies increasingly benefit from large-scale pretraining, but offline data alone is insufficient for robust real-world deployment. Deployed robots encounter distribution shifts, long-tail failures, task variations, and human correction opportunities that fixed demonstration datasets cannot fully capture. We present Learning While Deploying (LWD), a fleet-scale offline-to-online reinforcement learning framework for continual post-training of generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies. Starting from a pretrained VLA policy, LWD closes the loop between deployment, shared physical experience, policy improvement, and redeployment by using autonomous rollouts and human interventions collected across a robot fleet. To stabilize learning from heterogeneous, sparse-reward fleet data, LWD combines Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) for robust value estimation with Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) for policy extraction in flow-based VLA action generators. We validate LWD on a fleet of 16 dual-arm robots across eight real-world manipulation tasks, including semantic grocery restocking and 3--5 minute long-horizon tasks. A single generalist policy improves as fleet experience accumulates, reaching an average success rate of 95%, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [50] [Stereo Multistage Spatial Attention for Real-Time Mobile Manipulation Under Visual Scale Variation and Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00471)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Hyogo Hiruma,Masaki Yoshikawa,Hiroshi Ito,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于立体多级空间注意力深度预测学习的方法，用于实时移动操作。该方法从立体图像中提取任务相关空间注意力点，通过分层循环架构与机器人状态融合，实现闭环动作预测。在四种真实移动操作任务（刚性放置、铰接物体操作、可变形物体交互）中，相比模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，在随机初始位置和视觉干扰条件下表现出更高的鲁棒性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在开放非结构化环境中依赖机载视觉感知自主移动时，摄像头视角的连续变化会导致目标物体出现显著的视觉尺度变化，影响基于视觉的运动生成。

Method: 提出立体多级空间注意力深度预测学习方法：从立体图像中提取任务相关的空间注意力点，通过分层循环架构（hierarchical recurrent architecture）将注意力点与机器人状态融合，实现闭环动作预测。

Result: 在四种真实移动操作任务中，随机初始位置和视觉干扰条件下，相比模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，表现出更高的鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结构化的立体空间注意力结合预测性时序建模，在评估的移动操作场景中提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in open, unstructured real-world environments must rely on onboard visual perception while autonomously moving across different locations. Continuous changes in onboard camera viewpoints cause significant visual scale variations in target objects, affecting vision-based motion generation. In this work, we present a stereo multistage spatial attention-based deep predictive learning method for real-time mobile manipulation. The proposed methods extracts task-relevant spatial attention points from stereo images and integrates them with robot states through a hierarchical recurrent architecture for closed-loop action prediction. We evaluate the system on four real-world mobile manipulation tasks using a mobile manipulator, including rigid placement, articulated object manipulation, and deformable object interaction. Experiments under randomized initial positions and visual disturbance conditions demonstrate improved robustness and task success rates compared to representative imitation learning and vision-language-action baselines under identical control settings. The results indicate that structured stereo spatial attention combined with predictive temporal modeling provides an effective solution within the evaluated mobile manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [51] [MSACT: Multistage Spatial Alignment for Stable Low-Latency Fine Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00475)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Masaki Yoshikawa,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多阶段空间注意力模块，结合预训练ResNet视觉先验和自监督时序对齐损失，在ACT框架上提取稳定的2D注意力点作为局部空间模态进行动作预测，通过预测未来注意力序列抑制漂移，在ALOHA双臂平台上实现低延迟、稳定的精细操作，实验表明在定位稳定性和任务性能上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂精细操作策略存在不同权衡：动作分块策略（如ACT）依赖密集视觉特征、缺乏显式空间一致性；生成式方法（如Diffusion Policy）存在迭代采样延迟；VLA和体素方法计算成本高、系统复杂度大。在数据有限且标注成本高的情况下，需要一种兼顾低延迟、空间一致性和数据效率的方法。

Method: 基于ACT框架，引入多阶段空间注意力模块，利用预训练ResNet提取任务相关的2D注意力点作为局部空间模态；提出自监督时序对齐损失，将预测的注意力序列与未来帧视觉特征对齐，无需关键点标注即可抑制漂移，增强视觉到动作映射的稳定性。

Result: 在ALOHA双臂平台的仿真和真实精细操作任务上，评估了任务成功率、注意力漂移、推理延迟和对视觉干扰的鲁棒性。结果表明，在保持低延迟推理的条件下，定位稳定性和任务性能均得到改善。

Conclusion: 多阶段空间注意力模块结合自监督时序对齐能有效提升双臂精细操作中视觉定位的稳定性，在有限数据下维持低延迟推理，为实际应用中的稳定操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world fine manipulation, particularly in bimanual manipulation, typically requires low-latency control and stable visual localization, while collecting large-scale data is costly and limited demonstrations may lead to localization drift. Existing approaches make different trade-offs: action-chunking policies such as ACT enable low-latency execution and data efficiency but rely on dense visual features without explicit spatial consistency, generative methods such as Diffusion Policy improve expressiveness but can incur iterative sampling latency, vision-language-action and voxel-based methods enhance generalization and geometric grounding but require higher computational cost and system complexity. We introduce a multistage spatial attention module that extracts stable 2D attention points and jointly predicts future attention sequences with a temporal alignment loss. Built upon ACT with a pretrained ResNet visual prior, a multistage attention module extracts task-relevant 2D attention points as a local spatial modality for action prediction. To maintain consistent object tracking, we introduce a self-supervised objective that aligns predicted attention sequences with visual features from future frames, suppressing drift without keypoint annotations and improving stability of the vision-to-action mapping under limited data. Experiments on simulated and real-world fine manipulation tasks, conducted on the ALOHA bimanual platform, evaluate task success, attention drift, inference latency, and robustness to visual disturbances. Results indicate improvements in localization stability and task performance while maintaining low-latency inference under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [52] [Recovering Hidden Reward in Diffusion-Based Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00623)
*Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Yuting Hu,Shaokai Wu,Wenyuan Xie,Guodong Zhang,Qicheng He,Deyi Ji,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnergyFlow是一个统一生成式动作建模与逆强化学习的框架，通过参数化标量能量函数（其梯度作为去噪场），证明在最大熵最优性下，去噪分数匹配恢复的分数函数即专家软Q函数的梯度，无需对抗训练即可提取奖励。理论证明了保守场约束降低假设复杂度并收紧OOD泛化界，经验上在多种操控任务中达到SOTA模仿性能并提供优于对抗IRL和似然方法的奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习方法通常依赖对抗训练或似然最大化，存在训练不稳定、泛化性差等问题。本文旨在将生成式动作建模与IRL统一，通过能量函数的保守梯度场实现无需对抗训练的奖励提取，同时利用结构约束提升泛化能力。

Method: 1) 参数化标量能量函数，其梯度定义为去噪场；2) 通过去噪分数匹配学习分数函数，理论上证明其恢复专家软Q函数的梯度；3) 施加保守场约束（梯度为某标量函数的梯度），证明该约束降低假设复杂度并收紧OOD泛化界；4) 对奖励可辨识性和分数估计误差传播进行理论刻画。

Result: 在多种操控任务上，EnergyFlow在模仿性能上达到SOTA；作为奖励信号用于下游强化学习时，优于对抗IRL方法和基于似然的替代方法。

Conclusion: 有效奖励提取所需的结构约束（保守性）同时作为有益的归纳偏置促进了策略泛化，表明生成建模与IRL的统一框架具有理论和实践优势。

Abstract: This paper introduces EnergyFlow, a framework that unifies generative action modeling with inverse reinforcement learning by parameterizing a scalar energy function whose gradient is the denoising field. We establish that under maximum-entropy optimality, the score function learned via denoising score matching recovers the gradient of the expert's soft Q-function, enabling reward extraction without adversarial training. Formally, we prove that constraining the learned field to be conservative reduces hypothesis complexity and tightens out-of-distribution generalization bounds. We further characterize the identifiability of recovered rewards and bound how score estimation errors propagate to action preferences. Empirically, EnergyFlow achieves state-of-the-art imitation performance on various manipulation tasks while providing an effective reward signal for downstream reinforcement learning that outperforms both adversarial IRL methods and likelihood-based alternatives. These results show that the structural constraints required for valid reward extraction simultaneously serve as beneficial inductive biases for policy generalization. The code is available at https://github.com/sotaagi/EnergyFlow.

</details>


### [53] [Paired-CSLiDAR: Height-Stratified Registration for Cross-Source Aerial-Ground LiDAR Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00634)
*Montana Hoover,Jing Liang,Tianrui Guan,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出跨源空地LiDAR基准数据集CSLiDAR及无训练几何配准方法RGSR，通过高度分层ICP和置信门控策略在50米半径内实现地面扫描姿态精化，达到86.0%的S@0.75m精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地LiDAR扫描存在显著模态差异（航空扫描屋顶/冠层，地面扫描立面/冠下），两者共享几何极少（仅地表），导致标准配准方法和学习型对应模型收敛至度量错误局部极小值。

Method: 提出残差引导分层配准（RGSR）：高度分层ICP利用共享地面平面，结合反向配准方向和置信门控的接受-if-更好选择策略，无需训练且纯几何驱动。

Result: 在9012扫描主基准上达到86.0% S@0.75m和99.8% S@1.0m，优于置信门控级联（83.7%）和GeoTransformer（76.3%）。添加Fourier-Mellin BEV提议可降低RMSE但在极端部分重叠下增加实际位姿误差。

Conclusion: CSLiDAR为跨源空地LiDAR配准提供了标准化基准，RGSR证明了无训练几何方法的有效性，数据集和代码将公开。

Abstract: We introduce Paired-CSLiDAR (CSLiDAR), a cross-source aerial-ground LiDAR benchmark for single-scan pose refinement: refining a ground-scan pose within a 50 m-radius aerial crop. The benchmark contains 12,683 ground-aerial pairs across 6 evaluation sites and per-scan reference 6-DoF alignments for sub-meter root-mean-square error (RMSE) evaluation. Because aerial scans capture rooftops and canopy while ground scans capture facades and under-canopy, the two modalities share only a fraction of their geometry, primarily the terrain surface, causing standard registration methods and learned correspondence models to converge to metrically incorrect local minima. We propose Residual-Guided Stratified Registration (RGSR), a training-free, geometry-only refinement pipeline that exploits the shared ground plane through height-stratified ICP, reversed registration directions, and confidence-gated accept-if-better selection. RGSR achieves 86.0% S@0.75 m and 99.8% S@1.0 m on the primary benchmark of 9,012 scans, outperforming both the confidence-gated cascade at 83.7% and GeoTransformer at 76.3%. We validate RMSE-based pose selection with independent survey control and trajectory consistency, and show that added Fourier-Mellin BEV proposals can reduce RMSE while increasing actual pose error under extreme partial overlap. The dataset and code are being prepared for public release.

</details>


### [54] [Affordance Agent Harness: Verification-Gated Skill Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00663)
*Haojian Huang,Jiahao Shi,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Affordance Agent Harness，一种闭环运行时系统，通过证据存储、情景记忆检索、自适应路由器和验证器门控机制，统一异构技能以在开放场景中高效完成可供性接地，实现了比固定流水线更优的精度-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可供性接地系统使用固定流水线组合多种技能，无法适应实例间难度差异、缺乏中间错误恢复机制、不能复用重复对象的经验，导致精度低、推理成本高。

Method: 构建闭环运行时系统，包含：(1) 证据存储统一管理异构技能输出；(2) 情景记忆检索为重复类别提供先验；(3) Router自适应选择与参数化技能；(4) Verifier基于自一致性、跨尺度稳定性和证据充分性门控决策；(5) Judge融合累积证据与轨迹生成最终预测。

Result: 在多个可供性基准和难度控制子集上，相比固定流水线基线，在提升接地质量的同时减少了平均技能调用次数和延迟，实现了更优的精度-成本帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过自适应闭环推理代替固定流水线，能够根据实例难度动态调整计算资源分配，在有限推理预算下实现更高效的可供性接地。

Abstract: Affordance grounding requires identifying where and how an agent should interact in open-world scenes, where actionable regions are often small, occluded, reflective, and visually ambiguous. Recent systems therefore combine multiple skills (e.g., detection, segmentation, interaction-imagination), yet most orchestrate them with fixed pipelines that are poorly matched to per-instance difficulty, offer limited targeted recovery from intermediate errors, and fail to reuse experience from recurring objects. These failures expose a systems problem: test-time grounding must acquire the right evidence, decide whether that evidence is reliable enough to commit, and do so under bounded inference cost without access to labels. We propose Affordance Agent Harness, a closed-loop runtime that unifies heterogeneous skills with an evidence store and cost control, retrieves episodic memories to provide priors for recurring categories, and employs a Router to adaptively select and parameterize skills. An affordance-specific Verifier then gates commitments using self-consistency, cross-scale stability, and evidence sufficiency, triggering targeted retries before a final judge fuses accumulated evidence and trajectories into the prediction. Experiments on multiple affordance benchmarks and difficulty-controlled subsets show a stronger accuracy-cost Pareto frontier than fixed-pipeline baselines, improving grounding quality while reducing average skill calls and latency. Project page: https://tenplusgood.github.io/a-harness-page/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [Smart Profit-Aware Crop Advisory System: Kisan AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00133)
*Debasis Dwibedy,Avyay Nishtala,Pranathi Mukku,D Snehaja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kisan AI是一个智能利润感知的作物咨询系统，通过在标准农艺特征中增加市场价格变量，训练随机森林模型（准确率99.3%），并结合Facebook Prophet价格预测引擎、MobileNetV2病害检测模块及九语言AI聊天机器人，构建了一个可安装的渐进式Web应用，帮助农民做出经济可行的种植决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物咨询系统存在"经济盲目性"——仅优化生物产量而忽视市场价格，导致农民做出农学上合理但经济上不可行的决策。

Method: 在九特征基准数据集（七个标准农艺属性+市场价格变量）上训练随机森林分类器，与八个基线模型对比评估；将RF模型集成到多语言渐进式Web应用中，结合Facebook Prophet六个月价格预测引擎、MobileNetV2病害检测模块和基于Anthropic Claude API的九语言AI聊天机器人。

Result: 随机森林模型达到99.3%的最高准确率和最低对数损失，证实纳入市场价格作为预测特征既有效又有影响力。

Conclusion: Kisan AI通过全栈应用解决了作物咨询系统的经济盲目性问题，为印度农民提供了一个可安装的、统一的利润感知作物咨询平台。

Abstract: Modern crop advisory systems exhibit a critical limitation termed \textit{economic blindness}. These systems primarily optimize for biological yield, often overlooking market price, which can lead farmers toward agronomically sound yet financially unviable decisions. In this paper, we develop Kisan AI, a smart profit-aware crop advisory system that resolves the above-mentioned limitation through a research-driven, full-stack application. We train the Random Forest(RF) classifier model on a nine-feature benchmark dataset, the standard seven agronomic attributes augmented with a \textit{market\_price} variable, and evaluated against eight baseline models, considering the evaluation matrices, such as, accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Log Loss. The RF model achieves the highest accuracy of 99.3\% and the lowest Log Loss, confirming that the inclusion of market price as a predictive feature is both valid and impactful. We then implement the RF model within a multilingual progressive Web App alongside a Facebook Prophet six-month price forecasting engine and a MobileNetV2 disease detection module. A nine-language AI chatbot powered by the Anthropic Claude API unifies all modules into a single, mobile-installable platform accessible to farmers across India.

</details>


### [56] [High-Probability Convergence in Decentralized Stochastic Optimization with Gradient Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00281)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化随机优化中带梯度跟踪技术的DSGD算法（GT-DSGD）的高概率收敛性，在松弛的次高斯噪声下实现了非凸和Polyak-Łojasiewicz成本函数的阶最优收敛率，首次为结合偏差校正的去中心化方法提供了高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化优化中的高概率收敛结果几乎只针对DSGD算法，且需要强假设（如有限数据异质性或强凸性），这与MSE结果中偏差校正方法在更宽松条件下收敛的情况存在差距。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 将梯度跟踪技术融入DSGD（称为GT-DSGD），在松弛的次高斯噪声条件下分析其高概率收敛性能。对于非凸和Polyak-Łojasiewicz两类成本函数，分别推导出阶最优的高概率收敛速率。

Result: 非凸成本：$\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)$；Polyak-Łojasiewicz成本：$\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{nT}\right)$。两种速率均为阶最优，且与MSE意义下的收敛条件相同，瞬态时间相当。

Conclusion: GT-DSGD在高概率意义下与MSE意义下具有相同的收敛条件，结合偏差校正的优势在高概率意义上同样保持。这是首个为去中心化优化中结合偏差校正方法提供高概率保证的工作。

Abstract: We study high-probability (HP) convergence guarantees in decentralized stochastic optimization, where multiple agents collaborate to jointly train a model over a network. Existing HP results in decentralized settings almost exclusively focus on the Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\mathtt{DSGD}$) algorithm, which requires strong assumptions, such as bounded data heterogeneity, or strong convexity of each agent's cost. This is contrary to the mean-squared error (MSE) results, where methods incorporating bias-correction techniques are known to converge under relaxed assumptions and achieve better practical performance. In this paper we provide the first step toward bridging the gap, by studying HP convergence of $\mathtt{DSGD}$ incorporating the gradient tracking technique, in the presence of noise satisfying a relaxed sub-Gaussian condition. We show that the resulting method, dubbed $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$, achieves order-optimal HP convergence rates for both non-convex and Polyak-Łojasiewicz costs, of order $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{\sqrt{nT}}\Big)$ and $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{nT}\Big)$, respectively, where $n$ is the number of agents, $T$ is the time horizon and $δ\in (0,1)$ is the confidence parameter. Our results establish that $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ converges in the HP sense under the same conditions on the cost as in the MSE sense, while achieving comparable transient times. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first HP guarantees for decentralized optimization methods incorporating bias-correction. Numerical experiments on real and synthetic data verify our theoretical findings, underlining the superior performance of $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ and highlighting that the benefits of incorporating bias-correction are also maintained in the HP sense.

</details>


### [57] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedACT，一种面向多并发联邦学习任务的异构设备调度方法，通过对齐评分机制和公平参与策略，最小化平均任务完成时间（JCT），实验表明JCT降低高达8.3倍，模型准确率提升高达44.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究多聚焦于单任务优化，但实际应用需要多任务并行训练。直接套用单任务优化方法在多任务场景下因设备异构性和资源效率低下导致系统性能次优。

Method: 提出FedACT调度方法，核心包括：（1）对齐评分机制，评估设备可用资源与任务资源需求之间的匹配度；（2）参与公平性，确保设备在不同任务间均衡贡献；（3）优先分配高对齐分数的设备，同时保障跨任务公平参与，形成最优调度方案。

Result: 与最先进基线相比，FedACT将平均任务完成时间（JCT）降低高达8.3倍，模型准确率提升高达44.5%。

Conclusion: FedACT有效解决了多并发联邦学习场景中设备异构性和资源效率问题，显著提升了系统性能和模型精度。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [58] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理的可解释性框架，通过两个互补指标评估数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型是否真正学习到了底层物理规律，并发现低重建误差并不保证物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型能生成合理的微多普勒时频谱，但不确定模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律而非仅拟合数据分布。

Method: 提出两个互补的物理一致性指标：(1) 模型预测与物理导出的多普勒频率之间的对齐度；(2) 在速度干预下预测是否保持速度-频率关系。两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需实测雷达数据。

Result: 实验发现低重建误差不保证物理一致性：部分模型误差低但在两个物理指标上表现差。进一步分析表明时序注意力机制对Transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。

Conclusion: 仅凭低重建误差不足以判断模型是否学到物理规律，需要专门的物理一致性评估；时序注意力是Transformer模型捕获物理规律的关键组件。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [59] [Meritocratic Fairness in Budgeted Combinatorial Multi-armed Bandits via Shapley Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00762)
*Shradha Sharma,Swapnil Dhamal,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在预算组合多臂老虎机全带反馈（BCMAB-FBF）框架下实现 meritocratic 公平性的新方法，通过引入 K-Shapley 值来估计臂的贡献，并设计了 K-SVFair-FBF 算法，在理论上证明了 O(T^{3/4}) 的公平遗憾界，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全带反馈（full-bandit feedback）下无法获知单个臂的贡献，这使得公平性分配比半带反馈更具挑战性。现有工作缺乏针对 BCMAB-FBF 场景的公平性框架。

Method: 1) 将合作博弈论中的 Shapley 值扩展为 K-Shapley 值，用于刻画限于大小为 K 的集合中智能体的边际贡献，并证明了其满足对称性、线性性、零玩家性和效率性。2) 提出 K-SVFair-FBF 算法，该算法自适应地估计 K-Shapley 值，同时在未知估值函数下学习并缓解蒙特卡洛近似带来的噪声。

Result: 理论上证明了 K-SVFair-FBF 算法在公平遗憾上达到 O(T^{3/4}) 的界。在联邦学习和社会影响力最大化数据集上的实验表明，该方法在公平性和有效性上均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在 BCMAB-FBF 设置中引入了基于 K-Shapley 值的公平性框架，同时解决了全带反馈下的学习与噪声抑制问题，为公平性多臂老虎机提供了新的理论和方法基础。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for meritocratic fairness in budgeted combinatorial multi-armed bandits with full-bandit feedback (BCMAB-FBF). Unlike semi-bandit feedback, the contribution of individual arms is not received in full-bandit feedback, making the setting significantly more challenging. To compute arm contributions in BCMAB-FBF, we first extend the Shapley value, a classical solution concept from cooperative game theory, to the $K$-Shapley value, which captures the marginal contribution of an agent restricted to a set of size at most $K$. We show that $K$-Shapley value is a unique solution concept that satisfies Symmetry, Linearity, Null player, and efficiency properties. We next propose K-SVFair-FBF, a fairness-aware bandit algorithm that adaptively estimates $K$-Shapley value with unknown valuation function. Unlike standard bandit literature on full bandit feedback, K-SVFair-FBF not only learns the valuation function under full feedback setting but also mitigates the noise arising from Monte Carlo approximations. Theoretically, we prove that K-SVFair-FBF achieves $O(T^{3/4})$ regret bound on fairness regret. Through experiments on federated learning and social influence maximization datasets, we demonstrate that our approach achieves fairness and performs more effectively than existing baselines.

</details>


### [60] [RunAgent: Interpreting Natural-Language Plans with Constraint-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00798)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RunAgent 是一个多智能体计划执行平台，通过引入带有显式控制结构（IF、GOTO、FORALL）的智能体语言，结合约束推导验证、动态策略选择（LLM推理、工具调用、代码生成）以及上下文过滤机制，弥合自然语言表达性与程序确定性之间的鸿沟，在 Natural-plan 和 SciBench 数据集上优于基线 LLM 和 PlanGEN 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过执行有针对性计划来解决问题，但大语言模型在结构化工作流执行方面仍不可靠，现有方法无法同时兼顾自然语言的表达力与执行的确定性。

Method: 1. 提出带有显式控制结构的智能体语言（IF、GOTO、FORALL）；2. 基于任务描述和实例自动推导并验证约束；3. 动态选择 LLM 推理、工具调用或代码生成执行策略；4. 引入错误纠正机制；5. 执行每步时通过过滤上下文历史保留仅相关信息。

Result: 在 Natural-plan 和 SciBench 数据集上，RunAgent 的性能优于基线 LLM 和当前最先进的 PlanGEN 方法。

Conclusion: RunAgent 通过将自然语言计划转化为受约束的逐步执行流程，有效提升了 LLM 在结构化工作流执行中的可靠性与准确性。

Abstract: Humans solve problems by executing targeted plans, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for structured workflow execution. We propose RunAgent, a multi-agent plan execution platform that interprets natural-language plans while enforcing stepwise execution through constraints and rubrics. RunAgent bridges the expressiveness of natural language with the determinism of programming via an agentic language with explicit control constructs (e.g., \texttt{IF}, \texttt{GOTO}, \texttt{FORALL}). Beyond verifying syntactic and semantic verification of the step output, which is performed based on the specific instruction of each step, RunAgent autonomously derives and validates constraints based on the description of the task and its instance at each step. RunAgent also dynamically selects among LLM-based reasoning, tool usage, and code generation and execution (e.g., in Python), and incorporates error correction mechanisms to ensure correctness. Finally, RunAgent filters the context history by retaining only relevant information during the execution of each step. Evaluations on Natural-plan and SciBench Datasets demonstrate that RunAgent outperforms baseline LLMs and state-of-the-art PlanGEN methods.

</details>


### [61] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从公开事故报告中重建交通事故的参数化多模态学习方法，构建了包含6,217个真实事故案例的CISS-REC数据集，开发了语义到道路拓扑映射、车道一致运动重建和局部几何推理的框架，在重建保真度上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故记录以文本报告为主，但物理层面的精确事故重建因缺乏详细的场景测量数据和专家重建而困难、昂贵且难以规模化。现有方法难以从公开报告中自动、定量地重建事故过程。

Method: 1) 构建CISS-REC数据集（6,217例真实事故，来源NHTSA CISS）；2) 框架包含：报告语义到道路拓扑与参与者属性的映射、车道一致的事前运动重建、基于局部几何推理和时间分配的事故碰撞相关交互优化。

Result: 在CISS-REC上 outperform 代表性基线方法，取得最强的整体重建保真度，包括改进的事故点精度和碰撞一致性。

Conclusion: 公开事故报告可作为可量化验证的事故重建的可扩展计算基础，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [62] [CRADIPOR: Crash Dispersion Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00070)
*Edgar Chaillou,Sebastian Rodriguez,Yves Tourbier,Francisco Chinesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRADIPOR是一种数值离散度预测工具，利用秩降自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在汽车碰撞仿真后处理中识别对数值离散敏感的区域，无需重复计算，在实验中优于随机森林基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元碰撞仿真因并行计算和模型复杂性导致结果不可严格重复，后处理中性能指标的数值离散度影响工程决策，而通过重复仿真估算离散度计算成本过高，亟需一种低成本、可在常规后处理中应用的预测工具。

Method: 提出基于秩降自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类的框架，利用多种信号表示（如小波基、斜率变化）来识别对数值离散敏感的区域，并与随机森林基线方法进行对比。

Result: RRAE框架在数据集上优于随机森林基线；小波基和斜率变化输入表现最佳，其中斜率变化分类性能最优。

Conclusion: 结构化潜在表示有助于提升汽车碰撞后处理中数值离散度的检测能力，为在实际工程中替代重复仿真提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present CRADIPOR, a numerical dispersion prediction tool for automotive crash simulations. Finite Element (FE) crash models are widely used throughout vehicle development, but their predictions are not strictly repeatable because of parallel computation and model complexity. As a result, performance criteria evaluated during post-processing may exhibit significant numerical dispersion, which complicates engineering decision-making. Although dispersion can be estimated by repeating the same simulation, this approach is generally impractical because of its high computational cost.
  This work therefore investigates a prediction tool that can be applied during routine crash-simulation post-processing without repeating the computation. The proposed approach relies on a Rank Reduction Autoencoder (RRAE) combined with supervised classification in order to identify regions sensitive to numerical dispersion. The comparative analysis suggests that the RRAE-based framework is more effective than the Random Forest baseline on the studied dataset. Among the tested signal representations, wavelet-based and slope-based inputs appear to be the most promising, with slope variations providing the best classification performance. These results support the use of structured latent representations for improving numerical-dispersion detection in automotive crash post-processing.

</details>


### [63] [Hyperspherical Forward-Forward with Prototypical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00082)
*Shalini Sarode,Brian Moser,Joachim Folz,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF) 算法，通过将逐层二值拟合任务重构为超球面特征空间中的多类分类问题，将推理加速40倍以上，并在 ImageNet-1k 上首次实现超25% top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward (FF) 算法虽训练高效且受生物启发，但其推理过程需要为每个类别单独执行前向传播，计算成本极高，严重限制了实际应用。

Method: 将每层的局部目标从二值拟合任务重构为超球面特征空间中的直接多类分类问题，学习一组类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，使权重更新和推理可在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 相比原始 FF 算法推理速度提升40倍以上；在标准图像分类基准上达到更优准确率；在 ImageNet-1k 上实现超过25% top-1准确率（首次），迁移学习后达65.96%。

Conclusion: HFF 简单易实现，能有效扩展至现代卷积架构，在保持局部训练优势的同时显著缩小了与反向传播的性能差距，是贪心局部学习方法的重要突破。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm presents a compelling, bio-inspired alternative to backpropagation. However, while efficient in training, it has a computationally prohibitive inference process that requires a separate forward pass for every class that is evaluated. In this work, we introduce the Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF), a novel reformulation that resolves this critical bottleneck. Our core innovation is to reframe the local objective of each layer from a binary goodness-of-fit task to a direct multi-class classification problem within a hyperspherical feature space. We achieve this by learning a set of class-specific, unit-norm prototypes that act as geometric anchors and implicit negatives. This architectural innovation preserves the benefits of local training while enabling weight update and inference in a single forward pass, making it >40x faster than the original FF algorithm. Our method is simple to implement, scales effectively to modern convolutional architectures, and achieves superior accuracy on standard image classification benchmarks, closing the gap with backpropagation. Most notably, we are among the first greedy local-learning methods to report over 25% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k, and 65.96% with transfer learning.

</details>


### [64] [Introducing WARM-VR: Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Wearable Affect Recognition in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00184)
*Karim Alghoul,Faisal Mohd,Fedwa Laamarti,Hussein Al Osman,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WARM-VR，一个面向沉浸式VR环境的多模态情感识别公开数据集，包含31名参与者的生理信号（腕带BVP、EDA、温度、加速度和胸带ECG）及多感官刺激（视觉、听觉、嗅觉），通过放松海滩场景和算术压力任务诱发情绪，验证了嗅觉增强可显著降低负面情绪，并建立了多个机器学习基准模型（CNN、CNN-Bi-GRU、Transformer等），在效价和唤醒度二分类任务上取得F1约0.63-0.64、AUC约0.69的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别数据集多局限于静态环境，缺乏对沉浸式多媒体情境（如VR）的适用性，难以支持真实世界中多感官交互下的情感计算研究。

Method: 收集31名19-37岁参与者在VR环境中的生理信号（腕带：BVP、EDA、温度、三轴加速度；胸带：ECG），通过算术任务诱导压力后使用海滩场景（含视觉、听觉、嗅觉同步刺激）诱导放松，采用自评问卷和生理测量双重评估，并基于CNN、CNN-Bi-GRU、Transformer等模型进行基准测试。

Result: 问卷分析表明VR放松显著降低负面情绪，嗅觉增强效果更佳；BVP数据效价二分类中CNN和CNN-Bi-GRU最优（F1=0.63，AUC=0.69）；唤醒度分类中轻量Transformer表现最均衡（F1-0=0.54，F1-1=0.63）；放松任务中CNN-Bi-GRU达最高性能（平均F1=0.64，AUC=0.69）。

Conclusion: WARM-VR数据集为沉浸式多感官情感识别提供了有效基准，嗅觉刺激对情绪调节有显著增强作用，轻量Transformer在唤醒度分类中优于循环混合模型，证实了该数据集对VR情感计算研究的实用价值。

Abstract: With the growing integration of human-computer interaction into everyday life, advances in machine learning have enabled systems to better perceive and respond to users' emotional states. Most existing affect recognition datasets focus on static environments, limiting their applicability to immersive multimedia contexts such as Virtual Reality (VR). In this paper, we introduce WARM-VR, a novel publicly available multimodal dataset designed to support affect recognition in immersive, multisensory environments using wearable sensing instrumentation. Data were collected from 31 participants aged 19-37 using wearable sensors: a wristband measuring Blood Volume Pulse (BVP), EDA, skin Temperature, three-axis Acceleration, and a chest strap recording ECG signals. Participants engaged in immersive VR experiences designed to elicit relaxation through a calming beach environment following stress induction via an arithmetic task. These sessions incorporated synchronized multimedia stimuli: visual, auditory, and olfactory. Affective states were assessed subjectively through validated self-report questionnaires and objectively through the analysis of physiological measurements. Statistical analysis of the questionnaires confirmed that VR relaxation significantly reduced negative affect, particularly with olfactory enhancement. Furthermore, we established a benchmark on the dataset using widely recognized machine learning algorithms. The best performance for binary classification from BVP data of valence, was obtained with a CNN and a CNN-Bi-GRU model, both achieving an average F1-score of 0.63 and an AUC of 0.69. For arousal, a lightweight Transformer architecture provided the most balanced results (F1-0 0.54 and F1-1 0.63), outperforming recurrent hybrids. In the relaxation task, a CNN-Bi-GRU model reached the highest overall performance (average F1-score 0.64, AUC 0.69).

</details>


### [65] [Comparative Analysis of Polygon-Based and Global Machine Learning Models for Bus Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00083)
*Daniel Azenkot,Michael Fire,Eran Ben Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合空间聚类与多维特征分析的公交客流量预测框架，通过将城市划分为不同区域并为每个区域训练局部预测模型，达到了与全局模型相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型将整个城市视为单一同质区域，无法捕捉不同城区的独特和局部动态特征，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 利用公交客流量数据（按路线、时间、站点）以及开源数据（空间特征、气象条件、时间模式），基于邻近站点共享相似客流量特征的原则进行空间聚类，为每个聚类区域分别训练局部预测模型。

Result: 局部化方法达到了与全局模型相当的预测精度。

Conclusion: 空间感知的局部建模策略对于公共交通预测是有效的，能够为更具针对性和高效的服务改进铺平道路。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of bus ridership (passengers numbers) is crucial for efficient management and optimization of public transport systems. Traditional forecasting models often fail to capture the unique and localized dynamics of different urban areas by treating the entire city as a single, homogeneous region. This paper introduces a novel framework that enhances bus ridership prediction by integrating a spatial clustering methodology with multi-dimensional feature analysis. The proposed framework utilizes a diverse set of data, including bus ridership data (by route number, time, and bus stop) complemented by a variety of open source data, such as spatial features (e.g., attractive destinations), meteorological conditions (e.g., temperature, rainfall), and temporal patterns (e.g., time of day, day of week). By clustering the urban area into distinct regions, based on the principle that bus stops in close proximity share similar ridership characteristics, a separate local forecasting model is trained for each of these clusters. This localized approach demonstrates an accuracy comparable to that of global models. The findings suggest that a spatially-aware, localized modeling strategy is effective for public transport prediction, paving the way for more targeted and efficient service improvements.

</details>


### [66] [SPLICE: Latent Diffusion over JEPA Embeddings for Conformal Time-Series Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00126)
*Arnaud Zinflou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLICE是一个结合潜在生成式插补与自适应共形预测的模块化框架，在电力负荷数据上实现了高精度插补和分布自由、在线自适应的有限样本可靠性保证，流匹配变体在5-10步ODEs下达到与DDIM相当的质量，并具有跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补的生成模型虽重构精度高，但缺乏有限样本可靠性保证，这在以插补值指导调度与规划的电力系统中构成关键局限。

Method: 采用JEPA编码器将日负荷段映射至64维潜在空间；四种采样模式的潜在桥接生成候选轨迹；逐小时条件解码器还原信号空间；自适应共形推断（ACI）提供覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在13个负荷数据集上，SPLICE取得最低平均Load-only MSE (0.056)，在9/12非退化数据集上胜出，最佳CRPS为0.161（较最强基线低18.3%）；ACI实现93-95%经验覆盖，纠正静态共形预测最高7.5个百分点的欠覆盖；池化JEPA编码器经快速桥接微调即可迁移至4个未见领域。

Conclusion: SPLICE成功地将潜在生成式插补与分布自由的在线自适应预测区间结合，在保证高精度插补的同时提供了可证明的覆盖保证，且具备良好的跨域迁移效率。

Abstract: Generative models for time-series imputation achieve strong reconstruction accuracy, yet provide no finite-sample reliability guarantees, a critical limitation in power systems where imputed values inform dispatch and planning. We introduce SPLICE (Self-supervised Predictive Latent Inpainting with Conformal Envelopes), a modular framework coupling latent generative imputation with distribution-free, online-adaptive prediction intervals. A JEPA encoder maps daily load segments into a 64-dimensional latent space; a conditional latent bridge with four sampling modes generates candidate gap trajectories; an hourly-conditioned decoder maps back to signal space; and Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) wraps the output with coverage-guaranteed prediction bands. The flow-matching variant achieves comparable quality to DDIM in 5--10 ODE steps (5-10x speedup). On thirteen load datasets (nine proprietary, three UCI Electricity, ETTh1), SPLICE achieves the lowest mean Load-only MSE (0.056), winning 9/12 non-degenerate datasets at 91-day gaps and 18/32 across all gap lengths vs. five established baselines, and produces the best CRPS (0.161, -18.3% vs. the strongest competitor). ACI delivers 93--95% empirical coverage, correcting under-coverage failures of up to 7.5 pp observed with static conformal prediction. A pooled JEPA encoder trained on nine feeds transfers to four unseen domains, matching or exceeding per-dataset oracles with only a quick bridge fine-tuning.

</details>


### [67] [Learning Fingerprints for Medical Time Series with Redundancy-Constrained Information Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00130)
*Huayu Li,ZhengXiao He,Xiwen Chen,Jingjing Wang,Siyuan Tian,Jinghao Wen,Ao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对医学时间序列的自监督框架，通过交叉注意力瓶颈生成k个Fingerprint Tokens，并结合重构损失和TCR多样性惩罚实现解缠表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MAE）虽能有效预训练通用编码器，但未显式学习紧凑且语义可解释的潜在表示，通常依赖启发式聚合策略（如全局平均池化或[CLS] token）。

Method: 提出Fingerprint Tokens框架：使用交叉注意力瓶颈将可变长MedTS压缩为固定大小的k个潜在token；训练采用双目标函数——重构损失（确保token是原始数据的充分统计量）和基于总编码率（TCR）的多样性惩罚（显式最小化token间冗余，鼓励统计解缠）。

Result: 产生低维、可解释、样本高效的表示，每个token被鼓励捕获独立的变异因子，为更鲁棒的数字化生物标志物奠定基础。

Conclusion: 将该方法理论化为解缠率失真（Disentangled Rate-Distortion）问题，提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Learning meaningful representations from medical time series (MedTS) such as ECG or EEG signals is a critical challenge. These signals are often high-dimensional, variable-length and rife with noise. Existing self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Autoencoders (MAEs) are highly effective for pre-training general-purpose encoders. However, they do not explicitly learn compact and semantically interpretable latent representations, typically relying on heuristic aggregation strategies such as global average pooling or a designated [CLS] token. We propose a novel framework that compresses a variable-length MedTS into a fixed-size set of $k$ latent Fingerprint Tokens. Our architecture employs a cross-attention bottleneck to generate these tokens and is trained with a dual-objective function. The first objective is a reconstruction loss, which ensures the tokens are \textit{sufficient statistics} for the original data. The second, a diversity penalty based on the Total Coding Rate (TCR), explicitly minimizes the redundancy between tokens, encouraging them to become statistically \textit{disentangled} representations. We present the theoretical justification for our method, framing it as a novel \textbf{Disentangled Rate-Distortion} problem. This approach produces a low-dimensional, interpretable, and sample-efficient representation, where each token is encouraged to capture an independent factor of variation, paving the way for more robust digital biomarkers.

</details>


### [68] [Technical Report: Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ) for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00140)
*YiFeng Wang,Zhun Sun,Keisuke Sakaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARHQ是一种后训练权值分裂方法，通过构造激活残差Hessian矩阵并利用截断SVD分离误差敏感方向到高精度低秩分支，在Qwen3-4B模型上实现低比特量化下推理性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低比特激活-权值量化中误差传播导致模型性能严重下降的问题。

Method: 构建输入侧残差Hessian（基于激活量化残差G_x），对缩放后的权值矩阵W G^{1/2}_x进行闭式截断SVD，将误差敏感权值方向分离到高精度低秩分支中。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上，ARHQ显著提升了逐层信噪比(SNR)，并在ZebraLogic任务上保持了强量化条件下的下游推理性能。

Conclusion: ARHQ通过Hessian引导的权值分裂有效缓解了低比特量化中的误差传播，是一种有效的后训练量化改进方法。

Abstract: We present Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ), a post-training weight splitting method designed to mitigate error propagation in low-bit activation-weight quantization. By constructing an input-side residual Hessian from activation quantization residuals (G_x), ARHQ analytically identifies and isolates error-sensitive weight directions into a high-precision low-rank branch. This is achieved via a closed-form truncated SVD on the scaled weight matrix W G^{1/2}_x . Experimental results on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 demonstrate that ARHQ significantly improves layer-wise SNR and preserves downstream reasoning performance on ZebraLogic even under aggressive quantization. The code is available at https://github.com/BeautMoonQ/ARHQ.

</details>


### [69] [Consistent Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00161)
*Hasan Amin,Yuan Gao,Yaser Souri,Subhojit Som,Ming Yin,Rajiv Khanna,Xia Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多路径离散一致性模型（CDLM），通过训练去噪器在随机后验桥上保持路径不变性，实现单阶段、免教师的高质量离散文本生成，在少步采样下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续域中的一致性训练沿概率流ODE加速扩散，但离散扩散不存在样本空间ODE，无法直接适配。本文寻找离散域的自然替代方案——随机后验桥（精确封闭解），并基于此构建离散一致性模型。

Method: 提出Multi-Path Discrete Consistency (MPDC)原则，训练去噪器在多个随机后验桥上期望路径不变；实例化为CDLM，单阶段免教师训练框架，统一了掩码扩散、连续一致性模型和渐进/离散蒸馏。

Result: 在有条件和无条件文本生成任务上均取得新SOTA，在少步采样设置下增益最大，持续超越强基线扩散模型甚至多阶段蒸馏模型。

Conclusion: CDLM为快速、高保真的离散生成建模提供了有原则且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) are an attractive alternative to autoregressive models because they promise sublinear-time, parallel generation, yet practical gains remain elusive as high-quality samples still demand hundreds of refinement steps. In continuous domains, consistency training along the probability-flow ODE is a popular recipe to accelerate diffusion. For discrete diffusion, no analogous sample-space ODE exists, making direct adaptation ill-defined. We argue that the natural discrete substitute is not a deterministic trajectory but its stochastic counterpart: the exact posterior bridge, available in closed form for broad corruption families including masked and uniform diffusion. Building on this observation, we introduce Multi-Path Discrete Consistency (MPDC), a new principle that trains a denoiser to be path-invariant in expectation across these stochastic bridges, and instantiate it as the Consistent Diffusion Language Model (CDLM), a single-stage, teacher-free training framework. A single CDLM objective unifies masked diffusion, continuous consistency models, and progressive/discrete distillation as analytic limits or empirical approximations of one common view. Empirically, CDLM establishes a new state of the art on both conditional and unconditional text-generation, consistently outperforming strong base discrete diffusion models and often even multi-stage distilled baselines across sampling budgets, with the largest gains in the few-step regime. Together, these results position CDLM as a principled and scalable foundation for the next generation of fast, high-fidelity discrete generative modeling.

</details>


### [70] [Towards A Generative Protein Evolution Machine with DPLM-Evo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00182)
*Xinyou Wang,Liang Hong,Jiasheng Ye,Zaixiang Zheng,Yu Li,Shujian Huang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPLM-Evo 提出了一种进化离散扩散框架，在去噪过程中显式预测替换、插入和删除操作，通过解耦潜对齐空间与观测序列空间实现indel感知生成，并引入上下文进化噪声核以模拟真实突变模式，在蛋白质序列理解、突变效应预测（ProteinGym SOTA）以及可变长模拟进化等任务上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码的吸收扩散DPLM违背了蛋白质通过累积编辑逐步进化的生物学直觉，缺乏对替换和插入/删除操作的显式预训练目标，限制了优化式后编辑和灵活的条件生成。

Method: 1) 将扩散过程从观测序列空间解耦到上采样长度的潜对齐空间，实现indel感知生成；2) 引入上下文进化噪声核，产生符合生物进化模式的上下文相关突变模式；3) 在去噪过程中显式预测替换、插入和删除三种操作。

Result: 在ProteinGym单序列设定下取得最先进的突变效应预测性能；支持可变长模拟进化；能够通过显式编辑轨迹对现有蛋白质进行后编辑和优化，且计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: DPLM-Evo通过将进化操作注入扩散框架，更好地对齐了蛋白质进化的生物学直觉，在理解与生成任务上均展现出优势，为蛋白质语言模型的进化式设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Proteins are shaped by gradual evolution under biophysical and functional constraints. Protein language models learn rich evolutionary constraints from large-scale sequences, and discrete diffusion-based protein language models~(\eg, DPLMs) are promising for both understanding and generation. However, existing DPLMs typically rely on masking-based absorbing diffusion that contradicts a simple biological intuition: proteins evolve through accumulated edits, not by emerging from masks. Consequently, these frameworks lack explicit pretraining objectives for substitution and insertion/deletion (indel) operations, limiting both optimization-style post-editing and flexible guided generation. To address these limitations, we present DPLM-Evo, an evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that explicitly predicts substitution, insertion, and deletion operations during denoising. DPLM-Evo decouples an upsampled-length latent alignment space from the variable-length observed sequence space, which makes indel-aware generation tractable and enables adaptive scaffold growth throughout the process with negligible computational overhead. To better align substitutions with real evolution, we further introduce a contextualized evolutionary noising kernel that produces biologically informed, context-dependent mutation patterns. Across tasks, DPLM-Evo improves sequence understanding and achieves state-of-the-art mutation effect prediction performance on ProteinGym in the single-sequence setting. It also enables variable-length simulated evolution, and post-editing/optimization of existing proteins via explicit edit trajectories.

</details>


### [71] [Jailbroken Frontier Models Retain Their Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00267)
*Daniel Zhu,Zihan Wang,Jenny Bao,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 越狱攻击对模型能力的损害（“越狱税”）与模型能力成反比：越强大的模型在越狱后性能下降越少，最强越狱方法几乎不造成能力损失。Haiku 4.5平均损失33.1%性能，而Opus 4.6仅损失7.7%。推理密集型任务比知识回忆任务退化更严重。Boundary Point Jailbreaking实现了几乎完美的分类器规避且零退化。建议前沿模型的安全案例不应依赖越狱会导致有意义的能力退化这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究越狱攻击对不同能力水平的语言模型造成的性能影响（“越狱税”），验证越狱是否必然导致模型能力显著下降，为安全评估提供实证依据。

Method: 在Claude模型系列（Haiku 4.5到Opus 4.6）上评估28种越狱方法在5个基准测试中的表现，测量越狱前后的性能变化。

Result: 越狱税与模型能力成反比：Haiku 4.5损失33.1%性能，Opus 4.6仅损失7.7%。推理任务比知识回忆任务退化更严重。Boundary Point Jailbreaking实现近零退化。

Conclusion: 安全案例不应依赖越狱会导致有意义能力退化这一假设，因为最强模型和越狱方法已能实现近零能力损失。

Abstract: As language model safeguards become more robust, attackers are pushed toward developing increasingly complex jailbreaks. Prior work has found that this complexity imposes a "jailbreak tax" that degrades the target model's task performance. We show that this tax scales inversely with model capability and that the most advanced jailbreaks effectively yield no reduction in model capabilities. Evaluating 28 jailbreaks on five benchmarks across Claude models ranging in capability from Haiku 4.5 to Opus 4.6, we find Haiku 4.5 loses an average of 33.1% on benchmark performance when jailbroken, while Opus 4.6 at max thinking effort loses only 7.7%. We also observe that across all models, reasoning-heavy tasks display considerably more degradation than knowledge-recall tasks. Finally, Boundary Point Jailbreaking, currently the strongest jailbreak against deployed classifiers, achieves near-perfect classifier evasion with near-zero degradation across safeguarded models. We recommend that safety cases for frontier models should not rely on a meaningful capability degradation from jailbreaks.

</details>


### [72] [Fair Dataset Distillation via Cross-Group Barycenter Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00185)
*Mohammad Hossein Moslemi,Nima Hosseini Dashtbayaz,Zhimin Mei,Boyu Wang,Bissan Ghaddar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现数据集蒸馏会导致不同人口统计子群的表现差异，产生公平性问题；通过构建群体不平衡无关的预测信息重心来蒸馏共享表示，可有效减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法在压缩数据时忽略了不同子群预测模式的差异，导致模型在少数子群上性能显著下降，产生公平性差距，且简单平衡样本量无法解决该问题。

Method: 形式化分析了子群预测模式差异与样本量不平衡两种偏差源的交互作用，提出寻找群体不平衡无关的预测信息重心，通过蒸馏共享的聚合表示来确保所有子群获得相似的表示。

Result: 该方法与现有蒸馏方法兼容，实验表明能显著减少数据集蒸馏引入的偏差和公平性问题。

Conclusion: 数据集蒸馏中存在固有的公平性风险，根源在于子群预测模式差异而非样本量不平衡；通过蒸馏群体无关的共享表示可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Dataset Distillation aims to compress a large dataset into a small synthetic one while maintaining predictive performance. We show that as different demographic groups exhibit distinct predictive patterns, the distillation process struggles to simultaneously preserve informative signals for all subgroups, regardless of whether group sizes are mildly or severely imbalanced. Consequently, models trained on distilled data can experience substantial performance drops for certain subgroups, leading to fairness gaps. Crucially, these gaps do not disappear by merely correcting group imbalance, since they stem from fundamental mismatches in subgroup predictive patterns rather than from sample-size disparities alone. We therefore formally analyze the interaction between these two sources of bias and cast the solution as identifying a group-imbalance-agnostic barycenter of the predictive information that induces similar representations across all subgroups. By distilling toward this shared aggregate representation, we show that group fairness concerns can be reduced. Our approach is compatible with existing distillation methods, and empirical results show that it substantially reduces bias introduced by dataset distillation.

</details>


### [73] [Diversity in Large Language Models under Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00195)
*Roman Klypa,Oleksandr Cherednichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和大量实验验证了监督微调（SFT）会降低大语言模型生成多样性的现象，将其归因于微调数据集中低频模式的忽视和预训练知识的遗忘，并提出了Tempered Focal (TOFU)损失函数来同时解决这两个问题，在保持响应质量的同时提升输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT虽对对齐LLM与用户意图至关重要，但被认为会抑制生成多样性。尽管这一现象被频繁提及，相关实证研究却十分有限，已有方法的视角各异，表明更深入的分析能带来进一步改进。

Method: 通过理论分析将多样性下降归因于两个主要驱动因素：微调数据集中低频模式的忽视和预训练知识的遗忘；基于此提出Tempered Focal (TOFU)损失函数，同时解决这两个问题。

Result: 大规模实验证实了SFT后生成广度变窄的现象，并加强了对此效应的解释性假设。TOFU在多个模型和基准测试上提升了输出多样性，同时保持了高质量的响应。

Conclusion: TOFU提供了一种有原则的SFT方法，能够在保持高质量输出的前提下有效增强生成多样性，为SFT中多样性-质量权衡问题提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is essential for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with user intent, yet it is believed to suppress generative diversity. Although this reduction is frequently referenced, formal empirical testing of the phenomenon remains limited. The expressiveness of LLMs by itself was addressed by multiple prior methods. Their varying perspectives suggest that deeper analysis could yield further improvements. In this study, we attribute the decline to two primary drivers: the neglect of low-frequency patterns within fine-tuning datasets and the forgetting of preexisting knowledge. Motivated by our theoretical analysis, we develop Tempered Focal (TOFU) loss, a novel objective that addresses both stated challenges simultaneously. Our extensive evaluation confirms at scale that generation breadth narrows after SFT and strengthens the hypothesis explaining this effect. Across multiple models and benchmarks, we demonstrate that TOFU enhances output diversity while preserving high response quality, offering a principled approach to SFT.

</details>


### [74] [CompleteRXN: Toward Completing Open Chemical Reaction Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00222)
*Gabriel Vogel,Minouk Noordsij,Evgeny Pidko,Jana M. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompleteRXN基准，用于评估在真实缺失数据条件下化学反应补全方法。构建了对齐不完整与原子平衡反应的数据集，并评估了包括约束解码反应平衡器（CRB）在内的多种基线模型。CRB在随机分割上达到99.20%等价精度，在极端分布外分割上达91.12%，但所有方法在未 curated 的完整USPTO数据上性能显著下降，揭示了基准性能与实际鲁棒性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学反应数据集（如USPTO）普遍存在缺失副产物、共反应物和化学计量系数的问题，严重限制了其在下游应用中的可靠性与适用性。因此需要构建一个能真实反映缺失数据条件的基准，以推动反应补全方法的发展。

Method: 通过将USPTO记录与经整理的机理反应进行映射，构建对齐的不完整与原子平衡反应数据集CompleteRXN。提出约束反应平衡器（CRB），一种结合约束解码的编码器-解码器反应补全模型，并与SynRBL等算法方法进行对比评估。

Result: CRB在随机分割上达到99.20%等价精度，在极端分布外分割上达91.12%。SynRBL能生成平衡且化学合理的补全，但精度较低。所有方法随不完整度增加性能下降，且在未 curated 的完整USPTO上性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: CompleteRXN基准有效评估了反应补全方法的表现，但基准性能与实际鲁棒性之间存在显著差距，需进一步研究以提升方法在真实场景下的实用性。

Abstract: Chemical reaction datasets such as USPTO suffer from substantial incompleteness, frequently missing byproducts, co-reactants, and stoichiometric coefficients. This limits their applicability and reliability in downstream applications. Here, we introduce CompleteRXN, a large-scale supervised benchmark for reaction completion under realistic missing-data conditions. We construct a dataset of aligned incomplete and atom-balanced reactions by mapping USPTO records to curated mechanistic reactions. We evaluate representative baselines, including a novel encoder-decoder reaction completion model with constrained decoding, the Constrained Reaction Balancer (CRB), and a recent algorithmic method, SynRBL. On our CompleteRXN benchmark, the CRB achieves high performance across splits of increasing difficulty, reaching 99.20% equivalence accuracy on the random split and 91.12% on the extreme out-of-distribution split.
