Specifically, the generator is learned to produce coherent multimodal samples that serve as strong initial states for EBM sampling, while the inference model is learned to provide informative latent initializations for generator posterior sampling. Together, these two models serve as complementary models that enable effective EBM sampling and learning, yielding realistic and coherent multimodal EBM samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior performance for multimodal synthesis quality and coherence compared to various baselines. We conduct various analyses and ablation studies to validate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed multimodal framework.

</details>


### [116] [From Prediction to Practice: A Task-Aware Evaluation Framework for Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00645)
*Alireza Namazi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向任务的血糖预测评估框架，通过低血糖预警和胰岛素剂量决策两个下游任务，揭示了标准聚合指标（如低平均误差）无法反映模型在高风险场景（如餐后推注后）的严重失败，以及模型在反事实干预预测中的不足，证明了预测准确性与任务实用性之间存在系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准时序预测评估使用聚合指标（如MAE）无法反映模型在安全关键场景中的实际表现，低平均误差可能掩盖高风险区域（如低血糖事件）的危险失败，需要构建任务导向的评估框架来度量模型在下游决策中的真实效用。

Method: 构建双轨评估框架：（1）真实数据评估：基于三个临床队列，使用事件级召回率和每日误报次数评估低血糖预警性能，并特别分析餐后推注（胰岛素活性较高）这一高风险子集；（2）反事实干预评估：基于FDA认可的UVA/Padova模拟器，评估模型在配对事实/反事实场景中预测胰岛素方案改变后血糖响应的能力（方向、幅度、排序），并以临床成本函数评估胰岛素剂量选择质量。

Result: 整体召回率>0.9的模型在餐后推注子集上严重失败；在反事实任务中，真实数据预测表现良好的模型无法正确预测干预效应的方向、幅度和排序，且在临床成本评估下选择了较差的胰岛素剂量。

Conclusion: 预测准确性与任务实用性之间存在系统性且可重复的差距；标准时序预测评估不足以验证模型在安全关键临床决策支持中的适用性；提出的双轨评估框架及配套工具包（预处理流程、模拟器数据集）可作为可复现的基准。

Abstract: Clinical time-series forecasting is increasingly studied for decision support, yet standard aggregate metrics can obscure whether a model is actually useful for the task it is meant to serve. In safety-critical settings, low average error can coexist with dangerous failures in exactly the high-risk regimes that matter most. We present a task-aware evaluation framework for blood glucose forecasting built around two downstream uses: hypoglycemia early warning and insulin dosing decision support. For early warning, we evaluate on real data from three clinical cohorts using event-level recall and false alarms per patient-day, metrics that reflect operational alarm burden rather than aggregate accuracy. We show that models appearing acceptable overall, with recall above 0.9 on the full test set, can fail badly in the post-bolus slice, where insulin-on-board is elevated and missed warnings carry the greatest clinical consequences. Standard forecasting evaluation, however, does not test whether a model can reason about the effects of actions, a requirement for supporting insulin dosing decisions. We therefore add a second, interventional arm using the FDA-accepted UVA/Padova simulator, where we evaluate whether forecasters can predict glucose responses to altered insulin plans in paired factual/counterfactual scenarios. We show that models that look strong on real-data forecasting often fail to predict the direction, magnitude, or ranking of intervention effects, and choose poor insulin doses when evaluated under a clinically motivated cost. Taken together, the two arms reveal a consistent gap between forecasting accuracy and task-relevant usefulness. We release the benchmark, the standardized preprocessing pipeline for public cohorts, and the simulator-based interventional dataset as a reproducible toolkit.

</details>


### [117] [PEACE: Cross-modal Enhanced Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment for Robust Pediatric Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00647)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一个跨模态对齐框架，通过三轴临床语义分解、标签查询特征提取和课程门控优化，实现成人到儿童ECG诊断知识的迁移。在ZZU-pECG数据集上，零样本、50样本和全微调设置的AUC分别达到59.39%、79.03%和90.89%，在PTB-XL共享标签空间上达到96.65% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动儿科心电图诊断面临两大挑战：基于成人数据训练的模型存在显著的跨群体分布不匹配问题，而儿科标签数据通常稀缺。因此，需要一种能够将成人ECG知识有效迁移到儿科场景的方法。

Method: 提出PEACE框架，包含三个核心组件：1）三轴临床语义分解（tri-axial clinical semantic decomposition），对ECG信号进行多维度语义解析；2）标签查询特征提取（label-query feature extraction），基于标签查询机制提取判别性特征；3）课程门控优化（curriculum-gated optimization），渐进式优化训练过程。利用Gemini生成标签条件语义描述作为辅助训练监督，推理时仅需ECG信号。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG数据集上，零样本设置AUC为59.39%，50样本设置为79.03%，全微调设置为90.89%。在PTB-XL共享标签空间上达到96.65% AUC。

Conclusion: 结构化临床语义监督能够改善低资源场景下的成人到儿童ECG迁移效果，但在实际部署前仍需前瞻性临床验证和更明确的年龄感知建模。

Abstract: Automated pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) diagnosis remains challenging because models trained predominantly on adult data suffer from substantial cross-population mismatch, while pediatric labels are often scarce. We present PEACE (Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement), a structured cross-modal alignment framework for adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer. PEACE integrates tri-axial clinical semantic decomposition, label-query feature extraction, and curriculum-gated optimization to align transferable adult ECG representations with pediatric diagnostic targets. Since ZZU-pECG provides no paired clinical reports, we generate label-conditioned semantic descriptors using Gemini with concise clinical prompts and use them only as auxiliary training supervision; inference remains ECG-only. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE achieves 59.39%, 79.03%, and 90.89% AUC under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning settings, respectively, and reaches 96.65% AUC on the shared PTB-XL label space. These results suggest that structured clinical semantic supervision can improve low-resource adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer, while prospective clinical validation and more explicit age-aware modeling remain necessary before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [118] [AdaMeZO: Adam-style Zeroth-Order Optimizer for LLM Fine-tuning Without Maintaining the Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00650)
*Zhijie Cai,Haolong Chen,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaMeZO是一种零阶优化器，通过不存储Adam风格的一阶和二阶矩估计，在保持MeZO低GPU内存优势的同时，显著减少前向传播次数（最多减少70%），并超越MeZO的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeZO虽然通过仅前向传播的方式降低了微调LLM的GPU内存需求，但其对损失景观不敏感，导致收敛速度慢。直接应用Adam会因存储矩估计而三倍增加内存需求，破坏MeZO的内存优势。因此需要一种既能利用Adam的矩估计优势又不增加内存开销的优化方法。

Method: AdaMeZO是一种零阶优化器，核心创新在于采用Adam风格的一阶和二阶矩估计，但不在内存中显式存储这些矩估计值，从而避免了Adam的内存开销。

Result: 实验表明AdaMeZO在性能上超越MeZO，同时最多可减少70%的前向传播次数。轨迹可视化证实AdaMeZO能够适应不同的损失景观。

Conclusion: AdaMeZO成功地在MeZO的低内存优势与Adam的损失景观探索能力之间取得了平衡，为资源受限场景下的LLM微调提供了更高效的优化方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs is necessary for various dedicated downstream tasks, but classic backpropagation-based fine-tuning methods require substantial GPU memory. To this end, a recent work, MeZO, which relies solely on forward passes to fine-tune LLMs, significantly reduces GPU requirements at the cost of slower convergence due to its indifference to loss landscapes. Standard solutions, such as Adam, explore loss landscapes by estimating the first- and second-order moments and storing them in memory to guide the model's movement through dimensions with lower curvature and vice versa. However, directly applying Adam negates MeZO's advantage as it will triple the memory requirement. In light of this, we propose AdaMeZO, a zeroth-order optimizer that leverages Adam-style first- and second-moment estimates without maintaining them in memory. We present a theoretical analysis of AdaMeZO, corroborated by extensive experiments demonstrating AdaMeZO's performance, showing that AdaMeZO can outperform MeZO while requiring up to $70\%$ fewer forward passes. Trajectory visualizations affirm AdaMeZO's ability to adapt to diverse loss landscapes.

</details>


### [119] [Reinforcement Learning with Markov Risk Measures and Multipattern Risk Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00654)
*Andrzej Ruszczynski,Tiangang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对风险厌恶的有限时域马尔可夫决策问题，提出了一类称为小批量度量的马尔可夫相干风险度量，以及多模式风险厌恶问题的概念。基于这些概念，开发了特征基Q-learning方法，证明了后悔界为O(H²N^H√K)，并提出了经济的Q-learning版本。理论结果在随机分配问题和短期多臂老虎机问题上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风险厌恶的有限时域马尔可夫决策问题中，缺乏有效的风险度量方法来处理高维或复杂系统，现有方法计算复杂度高且理论保证不足。

Method: 提出小批量马尔可夫相干风险度量（mini-batch measures）和多模式风险厌恶问题概念，设计基于特征的多模式Q因子近似的Q-learning算法，并推导高概率后悔界。

Result: 证明了O(H²N^H√K)的高概率后悔界，其中H为时域长度，N为小批量大小，K为episode数。同时提出了简化策略评估步骤的经济版Q-learning方法。

Conclusion: 理论框架和算法在随机分配问题和短期多臂老虎机问题上得到验证，表明该方法在风险厌恶决策中有效且高效。

Abstract: For a risk-averse finite-horizon Markov Decision Problem, we introduce a special class of Markov coherent risk measures, called mini-batch measures. We also define the class of multipattern risk-averse problems that generalizes the class of linear systems. We use both concepts in a feature-based $Q$-learning method with multipattern $Q$-factor approximation and we prove a high-probability regret bound of $\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{ K}\big)$, where $H$ is the horizon, $N$ is the mini-batch size, and $K$ is the number of episodes. We also propose an economical version of the $Q$-learning method that streamlines the policy evaluation (backward) step. The theoretical results are illustrated on a stochastic assignment problem and a short-horizon multi-armed bandit problem.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluating the Architectural Reasoning Capabilities of LLM Provers via the Obfuscated Natural Number Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00677)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“架构推理”概念，并通过混淆自然数游戏（Obfuscated Natural Number Game）基准测试发现，通用模型在混淆环境下性能下降，而推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1、GPT-5等）即使缺乏语义线索仍能保持相同准确率，表明两者在数学推理能力上存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在形式化数学基准（如MiniF2F）上表现良好，但无法确定这些成就是源于真正的逻辑推理还是对预训练数据的语义模式匹配。因此需要设计一个消除语义线索的封闭环境，以区分模型是否具备真正的推理能力。

Method: 通过重命名Lean 4中自然数游戏的所有标识符，创建了一个零知识的封闭环境（Obfuscated Natural Number Game），用于评估模型在没有语义线索时的推理能力。

Result: 混淆操作普遍增加了推理时间（延迟税）；通用模型（Claude-Sonnet-4.5, GPT-4o）在混淆下性能显著下降，而推理模型（DeepSeek-R1, GPT-5, DeepSeek-Prover-V2）在缺乏语义线索的情况下仍保持相同准确率。

Conclusion: 混淆环境下的性能差异可以作为量化评估模型真正数学推理能力的指标，揭示了不同模型在架构推理能力上的本质分歧。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have achieved notable success on formal mathematics benchmarks such as MiniF2F, it remains unclear whether these results stem from genuine logical reasoning or semantic pattern matching against pre-training data. This paper identifies Architectural Reasoning: the ability to synthesize formal proofs using exclusively local axioms and definitions within an alien math domain, as the necessary ability for future automated theorem discovery AI. We use the Obfuscated Natural Number Game, a benchmark to evaluate Architectural Reasoning. By renaming identifiers in the Natural Number Game in Lean 4, we created a zero-knowledge, closed environment. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, finding a universal latency tax where obfuscation increases inference time. The results also reveal a divergence in robustness: while general models (Claude-Sonnet-4.5, GPT-4o) suffer performance degradation, reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, GPT-5, DeepSeek-Prover-V2) maintain the same accuracy despite the absence of semantic cues. These findings provide a quantitative metric for assessing the true capacity for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [121] [Deep Kernel Learning for Stratifying Glaucoma Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00708)
*Bruce Rushing,Angela Danquah,Alireza Namazi,Arjun Dirghangi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度核学习（DKL）和高斯过程（GP）的架构，利用transformer特征提取器和临床BERT嵌入，从多模态EHR数据中建模青光眼患者轨迹，成功识别出三个临床不同的患者亚组，其中高风险组虽平均视力较好但呈恶化轨迹，模型学会了区分疾病进展风险与当前严重程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性疾病（如青光眼）中对患者进行风险分层是重大临床挑战，临床医生需要从稀疏且不规则采样的EHR数据中识别高风险进展患者。

Method: 提出深度核学习（DKL）架构，采用高斯过程（GP）作为后端，GP的核函数由基于transformer的特征提取器定义，该提取器应用于临床BERT嵌入，以从多模态EHR数据中建模青光眼患者轨迹。

Result: 模型成功识别出三个临床不同的患者亚组；高风险组具有恶化轨迹但平均视力优于稳定差组，表明模型学会了识别进展风险而非仅当前疾病状态。

Conclusion: 基于风险轨迹进展进行患者分层的能力为临床决策支持提供了有力工具，可实现对高风险个体的针对性干预，改善青光眼护理管理。

Abstract: Effectively stratifying patient risk in chronic diseases like glaucoma is a major clinical challenge. Clinicians need tools to identify patients at high risk of progression from sparse and irregularly-sampled electronic health records (EHRs). We propose a novel deep kernel learning (DKL) architecture that leverages a Gaussian Process (GP) backend. The GP's kernel is defined by a transformer-based feature extractor applied to clinical-BERT embeddings to model glaucoma patient trajectories from multimodal EHR data. Our method successfully identifies three clinically distinct patient subgroups. Crucially, the model learns to decouple disease progression from current severity, identifying a high-risk group with a worsening trajectory despite having better average visual acuity than a second, stably poor group. This reveals that the model learns to identify progression risk rather than just the current disease state. This ability to stratify patients based on their risk trajectory progression offers a powerful tool for clinical decision support, enabling targeted interventions for high-risk individuals and improving the management of glaucoma care.

</details>


### [122] [SAVGO: Learning State-Action Value Geometry with Cosine Similarity for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00787)
*Stavros Orfanoudakis,Pedro P. Vergara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAVGO算法，通过学习状态-动作联合嵌入空间中的价值几何结构，利用相似性核引导策略更新，统一了表示学习、价值估计和策略优化，在MuJoCo连续控制任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示学习和相似性学习虽提升了RL样本效率，但很少被直接用于动作空间中的策略更新塑形，缺乏将价值信息几何结构显式融入策略更新的方法。

Method: 学习一个联合状态-动作嵌入空间，使得具有相似动作价值估计的样本对具有高余弦相似度，反之则映射到不同方向；利用该几何结构生成候选动作的相似性核，引导策略改进朝向更高价值区域，超越局部梯度更新。

Result: 在标准MuJoCo连续控制基准上，在高维任务中优于强基线方法；消融实验验证了价值几何学习和基于相似性的策略更新的各自贡献。

Conclusion: SAVGO通过几何一致的优化目标统一了表示学习、价值估计和策略优化，同时保持了off-policy actor-critic的可扩展性，为将价值几何结构直接用于策略更新提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While representation and similarity learning have improved the sample efficiency of Reinforcement Learning (RL), they are rarely used to shape policy updates directly in the action space. To bridge this gap, a geometry-aware RL algorithm that explicitly incorporates value-based similarity into the policy update, State-Action Value Geometry Optimization (SAVGO), is proposed. In detail, SAVGO learns a joint state-action embedding space in which pairs with similar action-value estimates exhibit high cosine similarity, while dissimilar pairs are mapped to distinct directions. This learned geometry enables the generation of a similarity kernel over candidate actions sampled at each update, allowing policy improvement to be guided directly toward higher-value regions beyond local gradient-based updates. As a result, representation learning, value estimation, and policy optimization are unified within a single geometry-consistent objective, while preserving the scalability of off-policy actor-critic training. The proposed method is evaluated on standard MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks, demonstrating improvements over strong baselines on challenging high-dimensional tasks. Ablation studies are done to analyze the contributions of value-geometry learning and similarity-based policy updates.

</details>


### [123] [Generating Statistical Charts with Validation-Driven LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00800)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Andraž Pevcin,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的结构化图表生成工作流，通过分解为数据集筛选、绘图提案、代码合成、渲染、验证驱动优化、描述生成和问答生成等步骤，并结合渲染输出验证，从74个UCI数据集生成了1,500张图表（涵盖24种图表类型）及30,003个问答对。在16个多模态大模型上的评估显示，图表语法类问题接近饱和，而数值提取、比较和推理仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM从表格数据生成图表时存在渲染后可见但数据或代码层面不可检测的失败模式（如可读性差、语义不匹配），且已有图表数据集缺乏完全对齐的工件（可执行代码、数据集上下文、问答对），因此需要一种结构化、可检查的图表生成流程。

Method: 提出结构化LLM工作流，将图表生成分解为6个阶段：数据集筛选、绘图提案、代码合成、渲染、验证驱动优化（基于渲染输出检测可读性/语义错误）、描述生成与问答生成。每个图表保留完整的代码、数据集上下文、描述和问答对。在74个UCI数据集上执行该流程。

Result: 生成了1,500张图表（24种图表类型）和30,003个问答对。在16个多模态大模型上的评估表明：图表语法类问题近乎饱和，但数值提取、比较和推理任务仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作流将图表生成从一次性提示-代码任务转变为可检查的流程，通过渲染验证解决了可视化特有的失败模式，生成的带标注图表数据集可用于诊断多模态大模型在图基推理方面的能力边界。

Abstract: Generating diverse, readable statistical charts from tabular data remains challenging for LLMs, as many failures become apparent after rendering and are not detectable from data or code alone. Existing chart datasets also rarely provide fully aligned artifacts, such as executable code, dataset context, and question-answer pairs. We present a structured LLM-based workflow that decomposes chart generation into dataset screening, plot proposal, code synthesis, rendering, validation-driven refinement, description generation, and question-answer generation. By incorporating rendered-output validation, the workflow addresses visualization-specific failure modes such as readability and semantic mismatch. It treats chart generation as an inspectable process rather than a one-shot prompt-to-code task, retaining each chart with its code, dataset context, description, and question-answer pairs. Applied to UCI datasets, the workflow produces 1,500 charts from 74 datasets, spanning 24 chart families and paired with 30,003 question-answer pairs. We evaluate 16 multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) on these chart-question pairs. The results show that chart-syntax questions are nearly saturated, while value extraction, comparison, and reasoning remain more challenging, illustrating the workflow's utility for diagnostic studies of chart-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Symbolic Execution Meets Multi-LLM Orchestration: Detecting Memory Vulnerabilities in Incomplete Rust CVE Snippets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00034)
*Zeyad Abdelrazek,Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合符号执行（KLEE）与4智能体多LLM架构的系统，用于检测Rust不安全代码中的内存漏洞。系统通过四个专用智能体协同从未编译的CVE代码片段生成KLEE兼容的测试工具，在31个真实Rust CVE上达到90.3%的包装编译成功率，检测出1,206个关键错误（83.9%检测率），显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化验证工具在处理CVE数据库中的不完整代码片段（缺少结构体定义、导入和Cargo清单）时完全失效，编译成功率为0%，需要一种能从碎片化代码中自动生成可执行验证工具的方法。

Method: 采用4智能体多LLM架构：Oracle/Validator（战略规划）、Safety Checker（漏洞分析）、Code Specialist（FFI包装生成）、Fast Filter（执行优化），协同合成KLEE兼容的测试工具。KLEE输出经graph_klee.py处理构建图数据库，实现跨CVE的结构化漏洞查询。

Result: 在31个真实Rust CVE（11个CWE类别）上，包装编译成功率90.3%（所有现有工具为0%），检测出1,206个关键错误（83.9%检测率），对比Clippy（35.5%检测率，14个警告）和Miri（仅通用标签）。4智能体架构将编译失败率从42%降至9.7%，检测错误从487增至1,206。

Conclusion: 角色专业化和结构化上下文传递比单一通用模型产生可测量的更优结果，系统能有效解决不完整代码问题并显著提升Rust不安全代码的漏洞检测能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a system combining symbolic execution (KLEE) with a 4-agent multi-LLM architecture for detecting memory vulnerabilities in Rust unsafe code. A central challenge we address is the incomplete-code problem: CVE database entries provide only isolated code snippets that lack struct definitions, imports, and Cargo manifests, causing all existing formal verification tools to fail at compilation with zero output. Our system resolves this through four specialized agents -- an Oracle/Validator for strategic planning, a Safety Checker for vulnerability analysis, a Code Specialist for FFI wrapper generation, and a Fast Filter for execution optimization -- that collaboratively synthesize KLEE-compatible harnesses from otherwise uncompilable fragments. KLEE's output is then ingested by graph_klee.py, which constructs a Graph Database linking CVE files, CWE categories, error types, and symbolic execution paths as typed nodes and labelled edges, enabling structured cross-CVE vulnerability queries. We evaluated our system on 31 real-world Rust CVEs spanning 11 CWE categories, achieving 90.3% wrapper compilation success where all state-of-the-art formal verification tools achieve 0%. Our system detected 1,206 critical errors across 26 files (83.9% detection rate), compared to 14 warnings across 11 files for Clippy (35.5%) and generic labels for Miri. The 4-agent architecture reduced wrapper compilation failures from 42% (single-agent baseline) to 9.7% and increased detected errors from 487 to 1,206, confirming that role specialization and structured context passing produce measurably better results than a single general-purpose model. Our replication package is publicly available at https://github.com/Zeyad-Ab/Symbolic-Execution-with-Multi-LLM-Architecture-for-Rust-Security

</details>


### [125] [Compliance-Aware Agentic Payments on Stablecoin Rails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00071)
*Kenneth See,Xue Wen Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种合规感知的智能体支付系统架构，通过将x402风格的签名授权与可编程合规机制（策略包装器+策略管理器）嵌入链上，在不牺牲效率的前提下确保人类不持续参与时的安全监管合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管环境中将智能体支付系统扩展至稳定币轨道时，需要确保人类不持续参与（非连续循环）的情况下，安全防护措施仍然有效。现有方案多依赖链下合规工作流，无法在自动化执行中保障合规性。

Method: 提出合规感知架构，结合x402风格的基于签名的支付授权和中继执行，通过策略包装器（policy wrapper）和策略管理器（policy manager）将可编程合规机制作为链上护栏嵌入，并协调模块化检查。合规在交易执行点强制执行而非作为链下独立工作流。

Result: 该架构在条件满足时保持低摩擦结算，记录交易关联的链上证明，并在合规要求待定时支持结构化决议流程。

Conclusion: 通过在执行点嵌入合规检查而非分离的链下流程，该方案在实现自动化支付执行的同时保障了监管合规性，为智能体支付系统的规模化应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Agentic payment systems extend delegated action to financial transfers, but scaling them on stablecoin rails in regulated settings requires safeguards that remain effective when humans are not continuously in the loop. We present a compliance-aware architecture that combines x402-style, signature-based payment authorisation and relayed execution with programmable compliance embedded as an on-chain guardrail via a policy wrapper and policy manager coordinating modular checks. By enforcing compliance at the point of execution, rather than as a separate off-chain workflow, the approach preserves low-friction settlement when conditions are satisfied, records transaction-linked on-chain attestations, and supports structured resolution when requirements are pending.

</details>


### [126] [zkSBOM: Privacy-Preserving SBOM Sharing with Zero-Knowledge Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00076)
*Tom Sorger,Eric Cornelissen,Aman Sharma,Javier Ron,Musard Balliu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出zkSBOM，一种基于零知识集的隐私保护SBOM共享机制，允许消费者查询漏洞而不泄露SBOM其他内容，并通过安全分析和真实场景评估验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM共享机制存在全公开或全隐藏的二元选择困境：软件供应商面临从SBOM推断出攻击信息的风险（如存在脆弱依赖），而消费者可能被篡改或虚假的SBOM所欺骗。

Method: 利用零知识集对SBOM组件进行密码学承诺，消费者可查询已知漏洞并接收加密证明，确认SBOM描述的工件是否受影响，同时不泄露其他SBOM内容。

Result: 通过安全分析量化了包含证明和排除证明的预期信息泄漏，并在真实场景中验证了zkSBOM的运行需求，证明其强安全性、隐私保护和可行性。

Conclusion: zkSBOM是一种强大、安全且保护隐私的SBOM共享机制，能有效保护软件供应商和消费者免受彼此的攻击。

Abstract: Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) are increasingly mandated by regulators, yet existing sharing mechanisms impose a binary choice between full disclosure and full opacity. This exposes software suppliers to attacks that can be deduced from the SBOM only, such as the presence of a vulnerable dependency. Conversely, software consumers can be fooled by software suppliers who modify or misrepresent published SBOMs. We present zkSBOM, a privacy-preserving SBOM sharing mechanism designed to address these threats. zkSBOM uses zero-knowledge sets to cryptographically commit to the components within an SBOM. Software consumers can query for known vulnerabilities and receive a cryptographic proof confirming whether the artifact described by the SBOM is affected, without revealing any additional SBOM content. We conduct a security analysis of zkSBOM by quantifying expected leakage from inclusion and exclusion proofs. We demonstrate real-world feasibility by applying it to realistic scenarios and evaluating its operation requirements. Our evaluation demonstrates that zkSBOM is a strong, secure, and privacy-preserving mechanism for SBOM sharing, protecting software suppliers and software consumers from one another.

</details>


### [127] [Alignment Contracts for Agentic Security Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00081)
*Isaac David,Marco Guarnieri,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"对齐契约"的框架，用于在可观察效应轨迹上指定和实施行为约束，以解决智能体安全系统中进攻能力范围控制的不对称问题。该框架定义了作用域、允许/禁止效应、资源预算和披露策略，并提供了有限轨迹语义、安全性质刻画、精炼与组合规则以及可判定的合规性检查。在效应可观察性假设下，给出了针对智能体模型的声音性定理和保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全系统将LLM规划器与漏洞发现、验证和报告工具相结合，产生了不对称控制问题：系统需要在授权范围内保留强大的进攻能力，同时在范围外必须禁止这些能力。现有防护措施提供策略控制，但未能将此边界作为可观察效应上的形式化契约。

Method: 提出对齐契约框架，为可观察效应轨迹指定行为约束；给出有限轨迹语义，将满足性刻画为具有有限违反见证的安全性质；开发精炼规则和单向组合规则用于模块化契约工程；证明合规性检查的可判定性；在Web聚焦的智能体安全工作流上实例化该框架。

Result: 建立了声音性定理（在效应可观察性假设下，对中介化效应给出保障，包括监视器实现轨迹的执行声音性）；提出了假设提升的适应结果；通过不可判定性转移和可观察性边界定理形式化了局限性；Lean 4形式化工具验证了核心定理。

Conclusion: 对齐契约为智能体安全系统提供了将行为边界作为一阶形式契约的框架，弥补了现有防护措施的不足，并建立了理论与实现之间的形式化桥梁。

Abstract: Agentic security systems increasingly combine LLM planners with tools that can discover, validate, and report vulnerabilities. This creates an asymmetric control problem: the system should retain strong offensive capability inside an authorized engagement, while the same capabilities must be denied outside scope. Existing guardrails provide useful policy controls, but they do not make this boundary a first-class formal contract over observable effects.
  We introduce alignment contracts, a framework for specifying and enforcing behavioral constraints over observable effect traces. A contract defines scope, allowed and forbidden effects, resource budgets, and disclosure policies. We give the language finite-trace semantics, characterize satisfaction as a safety property with finite violation witnesses, develop refinement and one-way composition rules for modular contract engineering, and show that admissibility checking is decidable. We instantiate the framework for web-focused agentic security workflows and show how the same structure extends to other effect profiles.
  Under an explicit Effect Observability Assumption, where all $\SigmaEff$-effects are mediated, the soundness theorem quantifies over the agent model and gives guarantees for mediated $\SigmaEff$-effects, including enforcement soundness for monitor-realized traces. We also state an assumption-lifted adaptation result and formalize limits through undecidability transfer and observability-boundary theorems. A Lean 4 artifact checks the formal core theorems used by the paper.

</details>


### [128] [Defense against Poisoning Attacks under Shuffle-DP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00625)
*Siyi Wang,Qiyao Luo,Yihua Hu,Lixu Wang,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对洗牌差分隐私（shuffle-DP）模型的通用防御框架，能够将所有保并查询（union-preserving queries）的shuffle-DP协议转化为抗投毒攻击的版本，在无攻击时保持渐近同等误差，在常数个攻击者时仅增加多项式对数级误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有shuffle-DP协议假设所有用户诚实，但现实中恶意用户可通过投毒攻击破坏隐私保护和结果效用；已有防御方案仅限于频率估计任务，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出首个通用防御框架，适用于所有保并查询，可将任意shuffle-DP协议转化为抗投毒攻击的版本，涵盖求和、频率估计、范围计数等常见查询。

Result: 无攻击时保持与原协议渐近同等误差；常数个攻击者时仅增加多项式对数级误差；实验验证了有效防御投毒攻击、保持高效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了shuffle-DP模型下通用抗投毒攻击防御的空白，在隐私、效用和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) has become the gold standard for protecting individual privacy in data analytics, and the shuffle-DP model has attracted significant attention from both academia and industry due to its favorable balance between privacy and utility. However, existing shuffle-DP protocols rely on a strong assumption: all users behave honestly. In real-world scenarios, adversarial users can exploit this vulnerability through poisoning attacks, compromising both privacy guarantees and the utility of analytical results. While defending against poisoning attacks in the shuffle-DP model has recently gained interest, existing solutions are limited to frequency estimation tasks. To address this issue, we propose the first general defense framework for all union-preserving queries, capable of transforming any shuffle-DP protocol into a version resilient to poisoning attacks. Beyond robust defense against poisoning attacks, our framework achieves high utility of analytical results. Compared to the original shuffle-DP protocol, it retains asymptotically equivalent error in attack-free settings and incurs only a polylogarithmic increase in error when a constant number of attackers are present. We demonstrate the generality of our framework on several common queries, including summation, frequency estimation, and range counting. Experimental results confirm that our approach effectively defends against poisoning attacks while maintaining strong utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [I can't recognize (yet): Delayed Rendering to Defeat Visual Phishing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00183)
*Ying Yuan,Cristiano Alex Rado,Giovanni Apruzzese,Mauro Conti,Luigi Vincenzo Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示视觉相似性检测的钓鱼网页防御系统存在时间差攻击漏洞：攻击者通过延迟渲染关键元素（如logo），使检测系统在截取快照时无法捕获完整视觉信息，从而绕过检测。实验表明该方法可使检测率从100%降至0%，且用户难以察觉。最后提出基于浏览器扩展的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉相似性检测的钓鱼网页防御方法虽然有效，但未考虑一种微妙且低成本的时间差攻击。这种攻击利用检测系统在渲染完成前截取快照的机制缺陷，绕过视觉检测模块，威胁实际部署中防御系统的可靠性。

Method: 1) 分析视觉钓鱼检测器的时间差攻击漏洞原理：检测器需获取目标网页快照并与合法网页进行视觉相似性计算；2) 提出攻击方法：攻击者延迟渲染关键元素（如logo），使快照截取时这些元素尚未完全加载，从而绕过视觉相似性检测；3) 实验验证：采用帘幕效果（curtain effects）等易于实施的问题空间技术，测试对最先进检测器的攻击效果；4) 用户研究：评估真实用户是否能识别这种扰动。

Result: 实验证明，利用时间差攻击可使最先进的视觉钓鱼检测器的检测率从100%骤降至0%。用户研究显示，真实用户无法可靠识别这些扰动（p<.05）。

Conclusion: 视觉钓鱼检测器存在固有漏洞，可被时间差攻击彻底绕过。提出缓解措施，包括一种无需调用远程服务、仅基于浏览器扩展的防御方案，用于警告用户可能已进入钓鱼网页。

Abstract: Phishing webpages are continuously polluting the Web. Plenty of countermeasures have been proposed and the most advanced techniques leverage machine-learning methods that infer whether a webpage is benign or not by inspecting its visual representation. Yet, despite the demonstrated effectiveness of such detection methods, this class of defenses is, by design, susceptible to a kind of subtle-but-cheap timing-based attacks which -- worryingly, and perhaps surprisingly -- have never been investigated so far. Such an oversight questions the overall reliability of these defenses in the wild.
  First, we show that timing-based evasion attacks have not been accounted for by prior work on visual phishing websites detectors. Then, we elucidate the intrinsic vulnerability of these detectors: they can be bypassed by delaying the rendering of webpage elements. Practically, these detectors must compute the visual similarity between a target webpage and a known legitimate one. This requires taking a "snapshot" of the target webpage before the similarity computation. Attackers can deliberately delay the rendering of key elements, such as the logo, so that these elements appear fully only after the snapshot has been taken. This simple tactic misleads the visual-similarity module, leading the system to incorrectly classify the phishing page as benign. We empirically show that state-of-the-art detectors can be completely defeated (detection rate dropping from 100% to 0%) by employing easy-to-apply problem-space techniques such as curtain effects. We also carry out a user study, evaluating the effectiveness of these attacks against real humans, and find that end users are unable to reliably identify our "perturbations" (p<.05). Finally, we propose mitigations, including a browser-extension that, without making any call to remote services, warns users that they may have landed on a phishing webpage.

</details>


### [130] [Skills as Verifiable Artifacts: A Trust Schema and a Biconditional Correctness Criterion for Human-in-the-Loop Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00424)
*Alfredo Metere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了Agent技能应被视为非可信代码，运行时必须默认执行验证而非依赖签名或来源信任，并提出了包含验证等级、能力门控、双条件正确性准则及十条规范指南的可移植运行时框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能作为LLM的一等部署制品，运行时加载技能时面临与操作系统/包管理器相同的信任问题：内容声称行为，运行时须决定是否信任。现有依赖签名、权限或注册表推断信任的方式不可靠，而人类在环（HITL）对每次不可逆调用的审查在规模上不可持续且退化为橡皮图章。

Method: 1. 信任模式（§\ref{sec:schema}）：在每个技能清单上显式标注验证等级。2. 能力门控（§\ref{sec:gate}）：HITL策略是验证等级的函数。3. 双条件正确性准则（§\ref{sec:biconditional}）：要求验证过程在对抗性集成练习（§\ref{sec:eval}）上满足充分必要条件。4. 可移植运行时概要（§\ref{sec:guidelines}）：基于开源参考实现提炼的十条规范指南。

Result: 提出了一个与模型和框架无关的运行时框架，所有组件无需重新训练、微调或专有基础设施。将技能验证作为独立的门控过程分离后，HITL仅对未验证的调用触发，系统变得可持续。

Conclusion: 核心主张：技能在验证前应默认视为非可信代码；信任应通过显式验证过程建立，而非通过签名、权限等级或来源注册表推断。这一方法可解决LLM技能运行时面临的根本信任问题。

Abstract: Agent skills -- structured packages of instructions, scripts, and references that augment a large language model (LLM) without modifying the model itself -- have moved from convenience to first-class deployment artifact. The runtime that loads them inherits the same problem package managers and operating systems have always faced: a piece of content claims a behavior; the runtime must decide whether to believe it. We argue this paper's central thesis up front: a skill is \emph{untrusted code} until it is verified, and the runtime that loads it must enforce that default rather than infer trust from a signature, a clearance, or a registry of origin. Without skill verification, a human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate must fire on every irreversible call -- which is operationally untenable and degrades into rubber-stamping at any non-trivial scale. With skill verification treated as a separate, gated process, HITL fires only for what is unverified, and the system becomes sustainable. We give a trust schema (§\ref{sec:schema}) that includes an explicit verification level on every skill manifest; a capability gate (§\ref{sec:gate}) whose HITL policy is a function of that verification level; a \emph{biconditional} correctness criterion (§\ref{sec:biconditional}) that any candidate verification procedure must satisfy on an adversarial-ensemble exercise (§\ref{sec:eval}); and a portable runtime profile (§\ref{sec:guidelines}) with ten normative guidelines abstracted from a working open-source reference implementation \cite{metere2026enclawed}. The contribution is harness- and model-agnostic; nothing here requires retraining, fine-tuning, or proprietary infrastructure.

</details>


### [131] [Zero-Knowledge Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00487)
*Pascal Berrang,Mirco Giacobbe,Jacob Swales,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合演绎模型检测与零知识证明的技术，能够在隐藏软件系统本身的前提下，形式化验证其满足公开的线性时序逻辑功能正确性规范。作者设计了显式状态和符号化两种方案，分别基于多项式承诺和Farkas引理与西格玛协议，并构建了原型验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要同时保证软件系统安全性和机密性的场景中，传统的模型检测要求系统完全公开，而现有零知识证明技术难以高效处理时序逻辑验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，使系统所有者能在不泄露系统内部实现的情况下，向外部验证者证明系统满足给定的时序规范。

Method: 采用演绎模型检测方法生成形式化正确性证书（如排序函数），再通过零知识证明协议向验证者证明该证书与保密的系统（转移关系）相匹配。显式状态方案将系统表示为转移图，使用多项式承诺隐藏转移关系；符号化方案将系统表示为线性守规命令，使用分段线性排序函数，通过Farkas引理构造公开/秘密成分的见证，再利用矩阵乘法和范围证明的西格玛协议完成零知识验证。

Result: 构建了原型系统，在线性时序逻辑验证实例上展示了两种方案的实际有效性，证明了所提技术在隐藏系统的同时能够完成时序规范的形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该技术使得在安全性和机密性均至关重要的领域（如商业机密软件、军事系统等）中能够进行形式化验证，扩展了模型检测的应用边界。

Abstract: We introduce a technology to formally verify that a software system satisfies a temporal specification of functional correctness, without revealing the system itself. Our method combines a deductive approach to model checking to obtain a formal certificate of correctness for the system, with zero-knowledge proofs to convince an external verifier that the system -- kept secret -- complies with its specification of correctness -- made public. We consider proof certificates represented as ranking functions, and introduce both an explicit-state and a symbolic scheme for model checking in zero knowledge. Our explicit-state scheme assumes systems represented as transition graphs. We use polynomial commitments to convince the verifier that the public proof certificates correspond to the secret transition relation. Our symbolic scheme assumes systems specified as linear guarded commands and uses piecewise-linear ranking functions. We apply Farkas' lemma to obtain a witness for the validity of the ranking function with public and secret components, and employ sigma protocols for matrix multiplication and range proofs to convince the verifier of the witness's existence. We built a prototype to demonstrate the practical efficacy of our two schemes on linear temporal logic verification examples. Our technology enables formal verification in domains where both the safety and the confidentiality of the system under analysis are critical.

</details>


### [132] [Attention Is Where You Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00236)
*Aviral Srivastava,Sourav Panda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力重分布攻击（ARA），通过识别安全关键注意力头并生成非语义对抗性标记来重定向注意力，以极少的标记和优化步骤绕过LLM安全对齐。实验发现安全机制并非局部化于特定注意力头，而是源于注意力路由过程，消融与重分布存在根本性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐LLM依赖RLHF和指令微调来拒绝有害请求，但其内部安全行为的实现机制仍不清楚。现有越狱方法多操作在语义或输出logit层面，缺乏对注意力几何结构的理解与利用。

Method: ARA是一种白盒对抗攻击方法：首先识别安全关键注意力头，然后利用Gumbel-softmax优化在概率单纯形上重定向softmax注意力分布，通过生成非语义对抗标记将注意力从安全相关位置转移开。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1和Gemma-2-9B-it上，ARA仅需5个标记和500步优化即可绕过安全对齐。在200个HarmBench提示上，Mistral-7B的ASR为36%，LLaMA-3为30%，Gemma-2为1%。关键发现：消融安全头最多导致1次拒绝翻转，而重分布攻击可翻转72/200（Mistral）和60/200（LLaMA-3）。

Conclusion: 安全行为并非局部化于特定注意力头作为可移除组件，而是源自这些头执行的注意力路由过程。移除一个头残差流可补偿，而重定向其注意力会向下游传播损坏信号。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models rely on RLHF and instruction tuning to refuse harmful requests, yet the internal mechanisms implementing safety behavior remain poorly understood. We introduce the Attention Redistribution Attack (ARA), a white-box adversarial attack that identifies safety-critical attention heads and crafts nonsemantic adversarial tokens that redirect attention away from safety-relevant positions. Unlike prior jailbreak methods operating at the semantic or output-logit level, ARA targets the geometry of softmax attention on the probability simplex using Gumbel-softmax optimization over targeted heads. Across LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1, and Gemma-2-9B-it, ARA bypasses safety alignment with as few as 5 tokens and 500 optimization steps, achieving 36% ASR on Mistral-7B and 30% on LLaMA-3 against 200 HarmBench prompts, while Gemma-2 remains at 1%. Our principal mechanistic finding is a dissociation between ablation and redistribution: zeroing out the top-ranked safety heads produces at most 1 flip among 39 to 50 baseline refusals, while ARA targeting the corresponding safety-heavy layers flips 72/200 prompts on Mistral-7B and 60/200 on LLaMA-3. This suggests that safety is not localized in these heads as removable components, but emerges from the attention routing they perform. Removing a head allows compensation through the residual stream, while redirecting its attention propagates a corrupted signal downstream.

</details>


### [133] [A Privacy-Preserving Approach to Conformance Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00283)
*Luis Rodríguez-Flores,Luciano García-Bañuelos,Abel Armas-Cervantes,Astrid Rivera-Partida*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于字符串处理算法和同态加密的隐私保护合规检查方法，允许在不暴露过程模型或事件日志的情况下进行对齐计算，尽管计算开销较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合规检查要求过程模型和事件日志对分析者可见，但在某些场景下，模型或日志包含敏感信息需要保密，因此需要一种既能进行合规检查又能保护双方隐私的方法。

Method: 基于对齐（alignments）这一合规检查的经典形式化方法，结合同态加密和字符串处理算法，使模型所有者和日志所有者在不向对方或第三方暴露各自数据的情况下完成合规性计算。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界事件日志上的实验表明，该方法能够安全地完成合规检查，但代价是较高的内存和计算资源需求。

Conclusion: 隐私保护的合规检查在技术上是可行的，但当前方案的计算效率仍有待优化。

Abstract: Conformance checking, one of the main process mining operations, aims to identify discrepancies between a process model and an event log. The model represents the expected behaviour, whereas the event log represents the actual process behaviour as captured in information systems records. Traditionally, the process model and the event log are both accessible to the business analyst performing the conformance checking. However, in some contexts, it is necessary to keep either the model or the log private to protect critical or sensitive information. In this paper, we propose a secure approach to conformance checking based on string processing algorithms and homomorphic encryption, where the process model and event log ar not visible to either the model's or event log's owner. The proposed technique is based on alignments, a well-known formalism used for conformance checking. An evaluation is performed using a synthetic and a real-world event log, showing that conformance checking can be securely computed at the expense of high memory and processing requirements.

</details>


### [134] [Trident: Improving Malware Detection with LLMs and Behavioral Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00297)
*Rebecca Saul,Jingzhi Jiang,Elliott Chia,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出结合大型语言模型的推理能力处理沙箱行为报告，生成基于行为的恶意软件检测规则，并构建了融合静态特征决策树、行为规则和LLM投票分析的Trident系统，有效应对概念漂移且无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PE恶意软件检测依赖静态特征（如字节直方图、字符串信息、PE头内容），但动态分析特征因沙箱行为报告的半结构化特性难以有效利用。作者希望利用最新一代具备推理能力的大语言模型来高效处理这些行为报告，将其纳入恶意软件检测流程。

Method: 1) 利用LLM基于少量标注恶意软件样本生成基于行为的检测规则；2) 提出Trident系统，融合三个组件：静态特征的经典决策树模型、基于行为的检测规则、以及对沙箱报告进行多数投票的LLM直接分析。

Result: 基于行为特征的检测规则相比标准静态特征方法对概念漂移具有更强的鲁棒性，同时保持实用的假阳性率。Trident系统优于仅用静态特征的标准方法，也优于单独使用行为规则，且在不需重训练的情况下达到与主动学习方法相同的抗概念漂移能力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以有效处理半结构化的沙箱行为报告并生成鲁棒的检测规则；融合多源信息的Trident系统在性能和抗概念漂移方面均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Traditionally, machine learning methods for PE malware detection have relied on static features like byte histograms, string information, and PE header contents. One barrier to incorporating dynamic analysis features has been the semi-structured nature of sandbox behavior reports. We show that, using the latest generation of large language models with reasoning, it is possible to efficiently process these behavior reports and utilize them as part of a malware detection pipeline. Specifically, we leverage LLMs to generate behavior-based malware detection rules based on a small training set of labeled malware. We find that these detection rules, derived from behavioral features, are much more robust to concept drift than standard static-feature methods, while maintaining practical false positive rates. Finally, we introduce Trident, a system which combines a classic decision tree model over static features, our behavior-based detection rules, and direct LLM analysis of sandbox reports through majority voting. Trident outperforms standard methods using static features, outperforms behavior-based rules alone, and is as resilient to concept drift as active learning methods without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [135] [Block-wise Codeword Embedding for Reliable Multi-bit Text Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00348)
*Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Dongsup Jin,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BREW框架通过将多比特水印从解码为中心转向指定验证范式，利用分块投票和窗口移位验证机制，解决了现有基于ECC的提取器存在的灾难性假阳性率问题，在10%同义词替换下达到TPR=0.965、FPR=0.02。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多比特水印方法优先考虑容量而非可靠性，解码与检测概念混淆，ECC提取器存在灾难性高假阳性率，且应用拒绝阈值会使得检测灵敏度坍缩至随机猜测水平。

Method: BREW框架包含两阶段机制：(i) 基于独立分块投票的盲消息估计；(ii) 窗口移位验证机制，严格验证载荷对局部编辑的鲁棒性。

Result: 在10%同义词替换攻击下，BREW实现了0.965的真阳性率和0.02的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 高假阳性率并非多比特水印固有的权衡问题，而是先前以解码为中心设计的结构性缺陷，BREW是模型无关且理论有依据的可靠取证部署方案。

Abstract: Recent multi-bit watermarking methods for large language models (LLMs) prioritize capacity over reliability, often conflating decoding with detection. Our analysis reveals that existing ECC-based extractors suffer from catastrophic false positive rates (FPR), and applying rejection thresholds merely collapses detection sensitivity (TPR) to random guessing. To resolve this structural limitation, we propose \textbf{BREW} (Block-wise Reliable Embedding for Watermarking), a framework shifting the paradigm to \emph{designated verification}. BREW employs a two-stage mechanism: (i) \textbf{blind message estimation} via independent block voting, followed by (ii) \textbf{window-shifting verification} that rigorously validates the payload against local edits. Experiments demonstrate that BREW achieves a TPR of 0.965 with an FPR of 0.02 under 10\% synonym substitution, demonstrating that the high-FPR issue is not an inherent trade-off of multi-bit watermarking, but a solvable structural flaw of prior decoding-centric designs. Our framework is model-agnostic and theoretically grounded, providing a scalable solution for reliable forensic deployment.

</details>


### [136] [CleanBase: Detecting Malicious Documents in RAG Knowledge Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00460)
*Weifei Jin,Xilong Wang,Wei Zou,Jinyuan Jia,Neil Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CleanBase是一种通过构建语义相似度图并检测文档团（clique）来识别知识库中恶意注入文档的方法，从而防御针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统容易受到提示注入攻击，攻击者将精心构造的恶意文档注入知识库，当用户提出相关问题时，检索到的恶意文档会误导RAG系统生成攻击者指定的答案，破坏系统完整性。

Method: CleanBase基于一个关键洞察：针对同一攻击目标问题而构造的恶意文档具有高语义相似性。该方法首先利用嵌入模型计算文档间的语义相似度，并基于统计确定的阈值构建相似度图；然后检测图中恶意文档形成的团（clique），将这些文档标记为恶意。

Result: 理论推导了CleanBase假阳性和假阴性率的上界。实验结果表明，在多个数据集和多种提示注入攻击下，CleanBase能够准确检测恶意文档并有效保护RAG系统。

Conclusion: CleanBase通过语义相似性聚类有效检测RAG知识库中的恶意注入文档，从根源上防御提示注入攻击，保障了RAG系统的输出完整性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, in which an adversary inserts malicious documents containing carefully crafted injected prompts into the knowledge database. When a user issues a question targeted by the attack, the RAG system may retrieve these malicious documents, whose injected prompts mislead it into generating attacker-specified answers, thereby compromising the integrity of the RAG system. In this work, we propose CleanBase, a method to detect malicious documents within a knowledge database. Our key insight is that malicious documents crafted for the same attack-targeted questions often exhibit high semantic similarity, as attackers deliberately make them consistent to improve attack success rates. Accordingly, CleanBase constructs a similarity graph over the knowledge database, where each node represents a document and an edge connects two nodes if their semantic similarity--computed using an embedding model--exceeds a statistically determined threshold. Due to their inherent similarity, malicious documents tend to form cliques within this graph. CleanBase detects such cliques and flags the corresponding documents as malicious. We theoretically derive upper bounds on CleanBase's false positive and false negative rates and empirically validate its effectiveness. Experimental results across multiple datasets and prompt injection attacks demonstrate that CleanBase accurately detects malicious documents and effectively safeguards RAG systems. Our source code is available at https://github.com/WeifeiJin/CleanBase.

</details>


### [137] [Pick and Sort for Graphical Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00558)
*Argianto Rahartomo,AmirHossein Jamshidipoor,Mohammad Ghafari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于"选择与排序"的图形化身份认证方案，用户通过选择并排列视觉元素完成认证，支持配置化参数和用户群体定制，原型实验表明易学易用但登录时间较长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本密码存在记忆负担和安全风险，亟需提供更直观、易用且可灵活配置的替代认证方案，尤其面向特定用户群体（如儿童）。

Method: 采用"Pick and Sort"交互范式：用户从视觉元素集中选择若干元素，并将其排列在网格中的指定位置；元素集和网格尺寸均可配置，支持针对不同用户群体定制视觉元素。

Result: 原型实现初步研究表明方案易于学习和部署，但登录时间比传统认证方式更长。

Conclusion: 该图形化认证方案在非时间敏感场景（如低频率访问或作为二次认证机制）中具有可接受性，但其登录效率仍需改进。

Abstract: We propose a graphical authentication scheme that follows a simple ``Pick and Sort'' design in which users choose visual elements and arrange them within a grid. Both the number of selected elements and the grid size are configurable, and the visual elements can be customized for specific user groups, such as children. A preliminary study with a prototype implementation indicated that the scheme is easy to learn and flexible to deploy. Although login times are longer than those of conventional authentication methods, the additional interaction may be acceptable in scenarios that are not time-critical, such as infrequent-access use cases or as a secondary authentication mechanism.

</details>


### [138] [KingsGuard: Enclave Data Protection Under Real-World TEE Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00613)
*Saltanat Firdous Allaqband,Deepanjali S,Rohit Srinivas R G,Devashish Gosain,Chester Rebeiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KINGSGUARD 是一种新型 TEE 设计方案，通过硬件支持的细粒度数据流追踪和控制，确保敏感数据不会离开飞地边界，并引入受控降级机制，在 RISC-V 处理器上实现了 10.8% 的硬件面积开销和 5.69% 的性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TEE 的安伞保障因飞地代码漏洞和底层硬件设计缺陷而受到削弱，即便有强隔离保证，敏感数据仍可能泄露。本文旨在弥合 TEE 理想威胁模型与实际实现之间的差距。

Method: 提出 KINGSGUARD，一种在硬件层面实施细粒度数据流追踪和检查的 TEE 设计，系统性地监控和控制在飞地内敏感数据的传播，并在飞地边界引入受控降级机制，允许有意图的数据释放。

Result: 在 RISC-V 处理器上实现，FPGA 综合后硬件面积开销为 10.8%，性能开销为 5.69%。

Conclusion: KINGSGUARD 通过硬件数据流追踪有效弥补了 TEE 理论安全模型与实际实现之间的差距，在合理开销下增强了安全性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have emerged as a cornerstone for securing sensitive computations by providing isolated enclaves protected from untrusted software. However, their security guarantees are undermined by vulnerabilities in both the enclave code and the underlying hardware design, which can allow sensitive data to leak despite strong isolation guarantees. This paper presents KINGSGUARD, a novel TEE design that systematically monitors and controls the propagation of sensitive data within an enclave. By enforcing fine-grained data flow tracking and checks in hardware, our approach ensures that sensitive data does not leave the enclave boundary, thus bridging the gap between the idealized threat models of TEEs and their practical realizations. Additionally, to balance security with practical functionality, we introduce controlled declassification at enclave boundaries, allowing intentional release of data to the outside world. Our implementation of KINGSGUARD on a RISC-V processor has a 10.8% hardware area overhead when synthesized on FPGA and a 5.69% performance overhead.

</details>


### [139] [STARE: Step-wise Temporal Alignment and Red-teaming Engine for Multi-modal Toxicity Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00699)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Tao Liu,Xiang Zheng,Hongying Zan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STARE是一个分层强化学习框架，通过将去噪轨迹视为攻击面，在白盒T2I和黑盒VLM设置下攻击视觉语言模型，实现了68%的攻击成功率提升，并揭示了"优化诱导相位对齐"现象——毒性语义在图像生成的不同阶段有可预测的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队方法将图像生成视为黑盒，只返回最终毒性分数，无法回答毒性语义在多步合成过程中何时以及如何出现的问题。

Method: 提出STARE框架，采用分层强化学习：高层提示编辑器修改文本提示，低层通过GRPO对T2I模型进行微调，在白盒T2I和黑盒VLM设置下将去噪轨迹本身作为攻击面。

Result: 相比最先进的黑盒和白盒基线方法，攻击成功率提升68%。发现优化诱导相位对齐现象：原始模型毒性分散，对抗优化将概念性危害集中在早期语义阶段，细节性危害集中在后期精修阶段。

Conclusion: 毒性形成从混沌过程转变为少量可预测的脆弱性窗口，既提供了强大的攻击引擎，也为基于相位的安全机制提供了基础。

Abstract: Red-teaming Vision-Language Models is essential for identifying vulnerabilities where adversarial image-text inputs trigger toxic outputs. Existing approaches treat image generation as a black box, returning only terminal toxicity scores and leaving open the question of when and how toxic semantics emerge during multi-step synthesis. We introduce STARE, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that treats the denoising trajectory itself as the attack surface, under a direct white-box T2I and query-only black-box VLM setting. By coupling a high-level prompt editor with low-level T2I fine-tuning via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), STARE attains a 68\% improvement in Attack Success Rate over state-of-the-art black-box and white-box baselines. More importantly, this trajectory-level view surfaces the Optimization-Induced Phase Alignment phenomenon: vanilla models exhibit diffuse toxicity, whereas adversarial optimization concentrates conceptual harms into early semantic phases and detail-oriented harms into late refinement. Targeted perturbations of either window selectively suppress different toxicity categories, indicating that this temporal structure is a genuine causal handle rather than a side effect of the hierarchical design. The phenomenon turns toxicity formation from a chaotic process into a small set of predictable vulnerability windows, providing both a potent attack engine and a basis for phase-aware safety mechanisms. Content warning: This paper contains examples of toxic content that may be offensive or disturbing.

</details>


### [140] [Self-Adaptive Multi-Agent LLM-Based Security Pattern Selection for IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00741)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Foutse Khomh,Carol Fung,Kawser Wazed Nafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPO，一种将LLM推理与确定性执行相结合的MAPE-K自适应多智能体安全模式选择框架，在资源受限的边缘IoT环境中实现100%无冲突激活、资源可行性保证，并将尾部延迟和能耗分别降低21.9%和23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘IoT运行时防御机制多依赖静态规则和 learned policies，无法在延迟、能量和计算约束下保证缓解动作的可行性、无冲突性和执行正确性。

Method: ASPO在MAPE-K控制环中分离随机决策生成与确定性执行：LLM智能体提出候选缓解组合，确定性优化核心强制执行封闭世界动作完整性、无冲突组合和资源可行性。

Result: 在分布式边缘网关测试台上，ASPO实现了100%无冲突激活、一致的资源可行性、完美等级保持的模式 dominance；深度决策探索将尾部延迟和能耗分别降低21.9%和23.1%，且不增加平均能耗。

Conclusion: 将LLM推理与确定性优化相结合的自适应安全模式选择方法，能够在资源受限的边缘IoT环境中有效兼顾安全性、资源效率和执行正确性。

Abstract: The adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) systems at the network edge of smart architectures is increasing rapidly, intensifying the need for security mechanisms that are both adaptive and resource-efficient. In such environments, runtime defence mechanisms are no longer limited to detection alone but become a resource-constrained task of selecting mitigation actions. Security controls must be carefully selected, combined, and executed under latency, energy, and computational constraints, while preventing unsafe interactions between controls. Existing approaches predominantly rely on static rule sets and learned policies, which provide limited guarantees of feasibility, conflict safety, and execution correctness in resource-constrained edge settings. To address this limitation, we introduce ASPO, a self-adaptive multi-agent security pattern selection that integrates Large Language Model (LLM)-based reasoning with deterministic enforcement within a MAPE-K control loop. ASPO explicitly separates stochastic decision generation from execution: LLM agents propose candidate mitigation portfolios, while a deterministic optimisation core enforces closed-world action integrity, conflict-free composition, and resource feasibility at every decision epoch. We deploy ASPO on a distributed edge-gateway testbed and evaluate it across two workloads, each comprising 500 and 1000 runtime security decisions, using replayed IoT attack traffic. In addition, the results demonstrate invariant safety properties, including 100% conflict-free activation, consistent resource feasibility across workloads, and stable pattern dominance with perfect rank preservation. Importantly, deeper decision exploration reduces extreme-case execution costs, compressing tail latency and energy overheads by 21.9% and 23.1%, respectively, without increasing mean energy consumption.

</details>


### [141] [Repurposing Image Diffusion Models for Adversarial Synthetic Structured Data: A Case Study of Ground Truth Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00788)
*Adam Arthur,Christopher Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测试了未经修改的Stable Diffusion U-Net直接应用于表格数据（UCI Adult Income数据集）生成合成数据的效果，并提出了两个哲学区分：统计真实性与感知真实性、合成证据与合成媒体，最后讨论了"真实漂移"现象——合成数据被重复使用后悄然被重新归类为真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证攻击者能否直接使用现成的公开图像扩散模型（而非专门训练表格数据生成器）来生成有效的表格合成数据，并揭示这类攻击在闭环证据链中可能导致的认知风险。

Method: 将UCI Adult Income数据集的每一行重塑为小型单通道伪图像，采用多种特征布局方案，直接使用未修改的Stable Diffusion U-Net进行处理和生成。

Result: 架构的归纳偏差使特征放置成为设计变量，且不同布局会影响生成效果；攻击者通过模仿接收端机器的思维方式可以成功生成合成证据。

Conclusion: 攻击者越成功，越能引发"真实漂移"——在未审查来源的流水线中，AI生成输出被悄然重新分类为真实数据，构成安全隐患。

Abstract: Public image diffusion models are now powerful enough that an attacker without the resources to train a tabular-specific generator may repurpose one off the shelf. This study tests that possibility directly. An unmodified Stable Diffusion U-Net is applied to the UCI Adult Income dataset by reshaping each row into a small single-channel pseudo-image. The architecture's inductive bias toward spatial locality makes feature placement a design variable, and several layouts are tested. However, this is only the beginning of the story, as this paper also draws two philosophical distinctions. One separates statistical from perceptual realism: whether synthetic content holds up to a machine's correlation audits or a human's sensory inspection. The other introduces synthetic evidence as a category alongside synthetic media: AI-generated material whose consumer is a machine in a closed evidentiary pipeline rather than a person in an open information system. An attacker succeeds with synthetic evidence by thinking like the machine that will receive it. And the more the attacker succeeds, the more they can induce ground truth drift: the silent reclassification of AI-generated outputs as authentic when reused in pipelines that do not interrogate their provenance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Cross-level Privacy Preserving Utility Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00036)
*Jiahong Cai,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘（CLPPUM）问题，提出了三种基于不同受害项选择策略的算法（Min-RF、Max-RF、Best-NSCF），并设计了GI-dic字典结构加速效用指标计算。实验表明，所提算法能成功隐藏所有敏感跨层级高效用项集且不引入人工项集，其中Min-RF在低最小效用阈值和稠密数据集下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护效用挖掘（PPUM）方法未考虑数据集中的分类学（taxonomic）信息，导致无法有效处理泛化项（generalized items）的识别与隐藏，因此需要研究跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘（CLPPUM）问题。

Method: 提出了三种CLPPUM算法：Min-RF（最小RGISU优先）、Max-RF（最大RGISU优先）和Best-NSCF（最佳NSC优先）；设计了GI-dic字典结构加速受害项识别和效用指标计算。

Result: 在多个数据集上，所提算法能成功隐藏所有敏感跨层级高效用项集，且不引入人工项集；在稀疏数据集上表现良好；Min-RF和Best-NSCF均优于Max-RF；Min-RF在低最小效用阈值和稠密数据集下性能最佳。

Conclusion: Min-RF算法在跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘任务中综合表现最优，尤其适用于低最小效用阈值和稠密数据集场景。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving utility mining (PPUM) aims to hide sensitive high-utility patterns while preserving the utility of the sanitized database. In practice, however, many datasets are associated with taxonomic information, which makes the identification and processing of generalized items more challenging. To address this, we investigate the cross-level privacy-preserving utility mining (CLPPUM) problem and propose a method for protecting generalized items. Based on different victim item selection strategies, we develop three CLPPUM algorithms: minimum RGISU first (Min-RF), maximum RGISU first (Max-RF), and best NSC first (Best-NSCF). Furthermore, to enable efficient victim item identification, a novel dictionary structure named GI-dic is designed to accelerate the computation of required utility metrics. Experimental results on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed algorithms successfully hide all sensitive cross-level high-utility itemsets without introducing artificial itemsets. The results also show that our method performs well on sparse datasets, and both Min-RF and Best-NSCF consistently outperform Max-RF. Overall, Min-RF achieves the best performance, particularly when the minimum utility threshold is low and the dataset is dense.

</details>


### [143] [SiriusHelper: An LLM Agent-Based Operations Assistant for Big Data Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00043)
*Yu Shen,Shiyang Liu,Qihang He,Yihang Cheng,Haining Xie,Zhiming He,Huahua Fan,Xianzhi Tan,Teng Ma,Shaoquan Zhang,Danqing Huang,Fan Jiang,Yang Li,Chongqing Zhao,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SiriusHelper是一个面向大数据平台部署的智能助手，通过意图识别、多跳检索（DeepSearch+优先级层级知识库）和自动化工单理解与SOP蒸馏，显著降低工单量20.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM+RAG助手在实际部署中面临场景覆盖有限、知识检索效率低（多跳检索不足、知识组织扁平化）以及维护成本高（工单非结构化、难以转化为可复用SOP）等挑战。

Method: 1) 统一在线助手自动识别用户意图并路由到正确处理路径（含专家工作流）；2) DeepSearch驱动机制+优先级层级知识库实现多跳检索，避免上下文过载；3) 自动化工单理解与SOP蒸馏，诊断助手失败原因并抽取领域特定SOP持续丰富知识库。

Result: 在腾讯大数据平台上的实验和在线部署显示，SiriusHelper优于代表性替代方案，并将在线工单量减少20.8%。

Conclusion: 通过意图路由、层级多跳检索和自动化知识蒸馏，SiriusHelper有效提升了大数据平台智能助手的场景覆盖、检索效率与可维护性，验证了在生产环境中的实用价值。

Abstract: Big data platforms are widely used in modern enterprises, and an in-production intelligent assistant is increasingly important to help users quickly find actionable guidance and reduce operational burden. While recent LLM+RAG assistants provide a natural interface, they face practical challenges in real deployments: limited scenario coverage across both general consultation and domain-specific troubleshooting workflows, inefficient knowledge access due to inadequate multi-hop retrieval and flat knowledge organization, and high maintenance cost because escalated tickets are unstructured and hard to convert into assistant improvements and reusable SOPs.
  In this paper, we present SiriusHelper, a deployed intelligent assistant for big data platforms. SiriusHelper serves as a unified online assistant that automatically identifies user intent and routes queries to the right handling path, including dedicated expert workflows for specialized scenarios (e.g., SQL execution diagnosis). To support complex troubleshooting, SiriusHelper combines a DeepSearch-driven mechanism with a priority-based hierarchical knowledge base to enable multi-hop retrieval without context overload, thus improving answer reliability and latency. To reduce expert overhead, SiriusHelper further introduces automated ticket understanding and SOP distillation: it diagnoses the assistant failure reason (e.g., missing knowledge or wrong routing) and extracts domain-specific SOPs to continuously enrich the knowledge base. Experiments and online deployment on Tencent Big Data platform show that SiriusHelper outperforms representative alternatives and reduces online ticket volume by 20.8\%.

</details>


### [144] [Multiset semantics in SPARQL, Relational Algebra and Datalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00417)
*Renzo Angles,Claudio Gutierrez,Daniel Hernández*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文研究了SPARQL模式在多集语义下的代数和逻辑结构，通过将其与Datalog和关系代数对齐，证明了三种形式化方法（非递归Datalog与安全否定扩展、多集关系代数、SPARQL）在多集语义下表达等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPARQL是多集（bag）语义的查询语言，但其代数与逻辑基础尚不明确。作者旨在通过将SPARQL与成熟的Datalog和关系代数对齐，揭示其底层结构。

Method: 将SPARQL（AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, SELECT）分别映射到（i）扩展了安全否定的非递归多集Datalog，（ii）包含投影、选择、自然连接、算术并和除操作的多集关系代数。证明三者表达等价。

Result: 证明了这三种形式化体系在多集语义下是表达等价的。

Conclusion: SPARQL的多集语义可以通过非递归Datalog与安全否定以及多集关系代数来统一刻画，为SPARQL的形式化提供了坚实的代数和逻辑基础。

Abstract: The paper analyzes and characterizes the algebraic and logical structure of the multiset semantics for SPARQL patterns involving AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, and SELECT. To do this, we align SPARQL with two well-established query languages: Datalog and Relational Algebra. Specifically, we study (i) a version of non-recursive Datalog with safe negation extended to support multisets, and (ii) a multiset relational algebra comprising projection, selection, natural join, arithmetic union, and except. We prove that these three formalisms are expressively equivalent under multiset semantics.

</details>


### [145] [EGREFINE: An Execution-Grounded Optimization Framework for Text-to-SQL Schema Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00628)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yu Pang,Rui Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EGRefine 是一个四阶段流水线，通过列级贪婪分解和基于视图的无损重写，对数据库模式中的模糊列名进行重命名优化，在保证查询等价性和列级不降级的前提下，提升 Text-to-SQL 的执行准确率，并支持跨模型复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据库模式常存在歧义、缩写或不一致的命名规范，导致 Text-to-SQL 模型准确率下降，现有方法将模式视为固定输入，仅在下游环节修正错误。本文希望在源头对模式进行优化。

Method: 将模式重命名形式化为约束优化问题，证明其计算复杂度，提出列级贪心分解；实现为四阶段流水线 EGRefine：筛选歧义列 → 生成上下文感知候选名 → 基于执行反馈验证 → 以无损 SQL 视图物化结果。验证阶段的保守选择保证列级不降级，视图物化保证数据库级查询等价性。

Result: 在受控模式退化、真实世界和企业级基准测试中，EGRefine 有效恢复了因模式命名噪声损失的准确率，并在任务超出当前 Text-to-SQL 能力时正确弃权；优化后的模式可跨模型家族迁移，实现一次优化、多模型服务。

Conclusion: EGRefine 提供了一种安全、有效的模式优化方法，能够提升 Text-to-SQL 系统在实际场景中的鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL enables non-expert users to query databases in natural language, yet real-world schemas often suffer from ambiguous, abbreviated, or inconsistent naming conventions that degrade model accuracy. Existing approaches treat schemas as fixed and address errors downstream. In this paper, we frame schema refinement as a constrained optimization problem: find a renaming function that maximizes downstream Text-to-SQL execution accuracy while preserving query equivalence through database views. We analyze the computational hardness of this problem, which motivates a column-wise greedy decomposition, and instantiate it as EGRefine: a four-phase pipeline that screens ambiguous columns, generates context-aware candidate names, verifies them through execution-grounded feedback, and materializes the result as non-destructive SQL views. The pipeline carries two structural properties: column-local non-degradation, ensured by the conservative selection rule in the verification phase, and database-level query equivalence, ensured by the view-based materialization phase. Together they make the resulting refinement safe by construction at the column level, with cross-column and prompt-level interactions handled empirically rather than analytically. Across controlled schema-degradation, real-world, and enterprise benchmarks, EGRefine recovers accuracy lost to schema naming noise where applicable and correctly abstains where the underlying task exceeds current Text-to-SQL capabilities, with refined schemas transferring across model families to enable refine-once, serve-many-models deployment. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/EGRefine.

</details>


### [146] [Living Databases: A Unified Model for Continuous Schema Evolution, Versioning, and Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00676)
*Amol Deshpande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一抽象框架，将数据库/数据集的更新、变换、版本控制、模式变更、流操作等多样功能整合到单一抽象和计算原语下，支持溯源追踪、条件传播、可配置变更通知，并提供派生对象（如视图、ML模型）演化的声明式控制机制，基于改进的Prolly Tree数据结构实现了原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将数据库演化相关的更新、变换、版本管理、模式变更、流操作等功能各自孤立研究，缺乏统一的抽象和计算原语，导致系统碎片化且难以支持新的应用场景。

Method: 提出统一的抽象框架，整合溯源追踪、条件传播和可配置变更通知；对派生对象（视图、ML模型）提供声明式演化控制机制；基于类关系数据库系统，采用改进的Prolly Tree（类Merkle树）数据结构实现原型，通过可调参数满足不同性能需求。

Result: 初步实验结果表明原型系统能够有效支持所提出的统一抽象框架的各项功能。

Conclusion: 该统一抽象框架能够涵盖现有用例并支持新的应用场景，提供了对数据集演化的系统性处理方法，有望替代传统各自孤立的解决方案。

Abstract: Databases, and datasets more generally, evolve continuously through updates, transformations, versioning, schema changes, streaming operations, and other mechanisms. While prior work has noted connections among some of these areas, they have traditionally been studied in isolation, each with its own abstractions, algorithms, and system implementations. In this paper, we argue for unifying these diverse functionalities under a single abstraction and a common set of computational primitives. We present such an abstraction, powerful enough to encompass existing use cases and to support new ones. Going beyond previous approaches, our framework seamlessly integrates provenance tracking for system-visible operations, conditional propagation of updates, and configurable alerts on change events. It also offers a principled treatment of dependent objects such as views and derived artifacts like machine learning models, by providing declarative mechanisms to control their evolution. Finally, we sketch a prototype implementation in a relational-like database system based on an adaptation of the "Prolly Tree", a Merkle tree-inspired data structure with tunable parameters to meet varying performance requirements, and present some initial experimental results.

</details>


### [147] [Complete Integration of Team Project-based Learning into a Database Syllabus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00736)
*S. Iserte,V. R. Tomas,M. Pérez,M. Castillo,P. Boronat,L. A. García*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Jaume I大学计算机科学专业高级数据库课程中采用团队项目式学习（TPBL）方法，持续四年（2018-2022）取得积极成效，并从团队评价、学生意见和最终成绩三个维度进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将项目式学习（PBL）与团队协作相结合，使学生在真实工作情境中通过团队合作完成实际项目，以提升学习效果并锻炼软技能与专业技能。

Method: 在高级数据库课程中实施TPBL，包括团队组建、活动设计、时间安排以及软技能和特定能力的训练，并从团队互评、学生主观反馈和课程成绩三个维度进行效果评估。

Result: 经过四年（2018/19至2021/2022学年）的实施，TPBL方法取得了积极成效。

Conclusion: TPBL能有效结合PBL与团队协作的优势，提升学生在真实工作情境下的项目开发能力和团队合作能力，三个维度的评估均支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Team project-based learning (TPBL) combines two learning techniques: project-based learning (PBL) and teamwork. This combination leverages the learning outcomes of both methods and places students in a real work situation where they must develop and solve a real project while working as a team. TPBL has been used in two advanced database subjects in Jaume I University (UJI)'s Computer Science degree program. This learning method was used for four years (academic years from 2018/19 to 2021/2022) with positive outcomes. This study presents the project development, which includes teamwork formation, activities, timetable, and exercised learning competencies (both soft and specific). Further, the project's results were evaluated from three different perspectives: a) teamwork evaluation by teammates, b) Students' opinions on the subject and project, and c) subject final grades.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Learning to Act and Cooperate for Distributed Black-Box Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00691)
*Zi-Bo Qin,Feng-Feng Wei,Tai-You Chen,Wei-Neng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC-MAS框架，通过大型语言模型从历史优化轨迹中生成稀疏高层指导，自适应地塑造智能体内部行为和外部协作模式，以解决分布式黑箱共识优化中手工规则与静态协作的局限，在异构非凸环境下显著提升解质量、收敛效率和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式黑箱共识优化方法依赖手工设计的更新规则和静态协作模式，难以在异构非凸环境中平衡局部适应、全局协调和通信效率，亟需一种能够从历史轨迹中自我设计优化策略的新范式。

Method: 首先重新设计智能体级群体动力学，引入自适应内部机制以平衡探索、收敛和局部逃逸；在此基础上提出LAC-MAS框架，利用大语言模型从历史优化轨迹中提供稀疏高层指导，同时塑造智能体内在动作行为和智能体间协作模式；并引入分阶段认知调度策略，以资源感知方式激活不同形式的适应。

Result: 在标准分布式黑箱基准测试和真实世界分布式任务上，LAC-MAS在解质量、收敛效率和通信效率方面均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了从手工设计分布式协调向自我设计多智能体优化系统转变的可行路径。

Abstract: Distributed blackbox consensus optimization is a fundamental problem in multi-agent systems, where agents must improve a global objective using only local objective queries and limited neighbor communication. Existing methods largely rely on handcrafted update rules and static cooperation patterns, which often struggle to balance local adaptation, global coordination, and communication efficiency in heterogeneous nonconvex environments. In this paper, we take an initial step toward trajectory-driven self-design for distributed black-box consensus optimization. We first redesign the agent-level swarm dynamics with an adaptive internal mechanism tailored to decentralized consensus settings, improving the balance between exploration, convergence, and local escape. Built on top of this adaptive execution layer, we propose Learning to Act and Cooperate (LACMAS), a trajectorydriven framework in which large language models provide sparse highlevel guidance for shaping both agentinternal action behaviors and agentexternal cooperation patterns from historical optimization trajectories. We further introduce a phased cognitive scheduling strategy to activate different forms of adaptation in a resource-aware manner. Experiments on standard distributed black-box benchmarks and real-world distributed tasks show that LAC-MAS consistently improves solution quality, convergence efficiency, and communication efficiency over strong baselines, suggesting a practical route from handcrafted distributed coordination toward self-designing multi-agent optimization systems.
